### 198 subject events

- **1914.01** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-coat-is-just-a-coat` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  Josef Bauer把Klara抱起来，走到衣钩下面，让孩子伸手去抓那件旧大衣。粗呢的袖口擦过掌心，Klara先愣住，随后皱着鼻子哭了一声。Josef Bauer笑着把大衣掀开，说“看，只有衣服”，灯影也跟着晃了一下。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +learned_the_coat_rack_

- **1915.09** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Josef Bauer，楼里的Emil Weiss正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Josef Bauer把裹着布条的Klara往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Franz Kral哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Klara蹲下把Franz Kral驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Franz Kral听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Klara的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Katharina Bauer回来接过孩子，Klara扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000292:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1918.04** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-mother-call` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  窗户上的玻璃响了一下，有人探出头，让Klara立刻回家。铁环还在Mitzi Lechner脚边，Klara蹲下去抽，抽不动。Mitzi Lechner说除非明天把铁钩带来。Klara点头，Mitzi Lechner把脚一抬，铁环滚到Klara手边，上面沾了一层鞋底灰。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.02_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Franz Lechner把Klara拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Klara把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Klara蹲下把两包并排，按Franz Lechner说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1919.12** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Mitzi Kral的鞋掉在井台边，Klara先去捡鞋，回头才看见Mitzi Kral摔在湿石头上。Berta Weiss用围裙裹住Mitzi Kral，血从膝盖渗出来。Katharina Bauer夜里把Klara叫到灶台前，数落了很久，最后把锅里剩下的半勺汤倒回大锅，只给Klara留了半碗。
  _effects: mood -0.06; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1920.02** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Klara后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Klara没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Klara用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Klara拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Emil Weiss弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Klara接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  木窗“咔”地合上，Klara的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Klara在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  _effects: cash -10 (青年会远足车费); relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.03_

  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; skill; mood -0.04999999999999999; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Katharina Bauer 2把Klara叫到巷口，问Mitzi Kral叫什么、住在哪间屋。Klara答到一半就停住了。Katharina Bauer 2说这半天的活不算数，说冒名顶替坏了规矩。Klara回到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时，Mitzi Kral正扶着床沿站着，听完只说“别去了”，说完又低下头，像是自己连累了Klara。

- **1927.12** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pass-it-on` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Grete Stein把一本拉丁文课本推到Klara面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Klara翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Klara把纸抄了一遍，塞进Mitzi的历史作业本；那天晚上，Klara第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。
  _effects: knowledge “配给数字与官方公告并不一致”; flag +yf18_read_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; mood 0.03_

  教堂年历的订单催得紧，Leopoldstädter Volksschule把压印机的护板卸了，说这样进纸快一半。Klara看着那块铁板，说手会进去；Leopoldstädter Volksschule把工钱袋往桌上一扔，问Klara是不是不想出师了。Klara没有碰机器，那天下午的活由Josef Kral接手。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 5; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; flag +yf18_refused_unguarded_press; mood 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Josefine Hofer把Klara叫到巷口，问Franz Kral叫什么、住在哪间屋。Klara答到一半就停住了。Josefine Hofer说这半天的活不算数，说冒名顶替坏了规矩。Klara回到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时，Franz Kral正扶着床沿站着，听完只说“别去了”，说完又低下头，像是自己连累了Klara。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000292:child; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西)_

- **1928.02** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Klara把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josef Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Klara那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

  墙上出现了一行关于Klara的脏话，Grete Stein让全班提水刷墙，刷了三遍还能看见白印。Emil在背后学那句话的腔调，Klara回头时，他正低头抠桌角的漆。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; condition; flag +target_of_sexual_rumor; relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

  第二天，Klara在教室里把滑道说成三层楼高、底下是鳄鱼池。Hedwig配合着补充细节，连Maria Moser的灯都被说成探照灯；全班听得入神，只有后排的Mitzi问了一句“那你为什么裤子破了”。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; mood 0.02999999999999997; flag +yf18_prater_story; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation 0.03; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  制图室里全是铅笔划纸的声音，尺子压住纸边，他的手心把纸角压出了汗渍。收卷时他把错的地方又看了一遍，才把纸递出去。回家路上他把图纸卷成筒，握在手里不松。
  _effects: cash -3 (技术学院考试报名费); mood -0.14999999999999997; skill; flag +kept_failed_drawing; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

  邻居不肯在收入表上签字，说“签了就得罪人”。Maria Moser按规定把申请退回，Klara的奖学金停了一学期。Josef Bauer在楼道里骂了一句，邻居家的门始终没有开。Klara去教务处问能不能补交，窗口说“等下一年”。
  _effects: cash -6 (奖学金复核表); flag +scholarship_suspended_for_missing_testimony; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1929.11** `r2-leisure.maid-follower::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/let-him-into-the-cellar` tags: domestic_service, courtship, servant, followers, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  地窖里堆着煤和酸菜桶，两人站在楼梯下面，Anna Bauer把一枚纽扣塞进Klara手里，说是军装上的，掉了还能再缝。Alois Berg的钥匙在楼上响，Klara把纽扣含在嘴里，出去时脸很红。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +let_follower_into_cellar; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; knowledge “the housekeeper checks the cellar door after dark”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1930.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Klara把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Klara读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

  Klara等了一整天，代表团没有来。傍晚，Klara在窗户里看见游行队伍从街口经过，新的“国王”骑着借来的白马，朝人群挥手。Klara把订做的戏服退掉，押金没有拿回来。
  _effects: cash -2 (订做戏服的押金); mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +wish_crown_failed_

  Klara在车行谈好一辆板车，又去新住处交了押金。Emil Berg把收据写得极简，只写“收到五克朗”，连地址都省了。Klara把收据折好，和户口纸一起放进内袋。

  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Klara没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; state_add political-suspect state:world-vienna-1912:188_

  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Klara把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Mitzi Lechner 2替Klara扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。

  _effects: cash 6 (补助金窗口的第二张表); flag +work13_unemployment_benefit_approved; knowledge “the benefit runs for twelve weeks and must be collected in p”_

- **1945.04** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  煤店老板用铁铲往筐里添了两铲碎煤，煤屑沾在Klara的袖口。她拎回来时筐底漏出几粒黑渣，落在楼梯上。火柴在窗台晾了一会儿，终于擦出一道火星。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); health -0.010000000000000002 (生火时被烟呛到); food_

- **1945.05** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Klara在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Klara去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1945.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Klara就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Josef摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状)_

  Klara的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Klara看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1946.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Elisabeth Hofer把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Klara留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Klara在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06000000000000005; role_

  战后新设的失业登记处在一间旧学校里，课桌当柜台用。Luise Kral问Klara战前做什么，又问他有没有被征召。Klara把退伍证明和工厂解雇单一起递过去，Luise Kral在两张纸之间来回看了三遍，最后批了十六周。
  _effects: cash 8 (补助金窗口的第二张表); flag +work13_postwar_benefit_granted; knowledge “the postwar benefit runs longer for returning soldiers”_

  _effects: cash -2 (按涨租纸条多付的两克朗（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-123_

  Luise Kral只要Klara在一张空白委任状上写下真名，说剩下的由办事处去办。一个月过去，职位没有来，Klara的名字却出现在一份领款单上，金额和签名都不属于Klara。Luise Kral调走了，窗口换了人，没有人愿意查那张纸是从哪一叠里抽出来的。
  _effects: cash -2 (打点办事处的钱（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +name_used_without_office; reputation -0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  Klara把圣马克斯殡葬合作社的名单藏进缝纫机抽屉的夹层。名单上有二十几个名字，其中两个已经搬走，一个被工厂开除。Klara没有把最后一行划掉。
  _effects: reputation 0.03_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

  _effects: cash -2 (订做戏服的押金（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

  _effects: reputation 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-123_

  Klara请同住的Maria Stein跑一趟，把Katharina Kraus约到教堂前的台阶上。石台阶还带着晨露，两人各占一级，把原信从头念完；念到“工钱”那处，Katharina Kraus说那是家里人的意思，不是自己的话。Klara把信纸折回原样，至少这一回没有隔着别人传话。

  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth)_

- **1950.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, injury
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Anton Winter在楼梯间坐了很久；Klara在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Klara的名字；此后邻居提起Klara，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: health -0.05999999999999999 (stillbirth); cash -6 (midwife and burial); role_

  这一年没有发生什么大事，只是Klara常常觉得累。柜子里的一只碗被碰掉了，Klara把碎片扫起来，没有扔，放在抽屉最里面。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +ordinary_sad_year_

- **1952.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/flat-allocated` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, household
  等了三年，住房局终于给Klara一张分配单：利奥波德城一栋战后修复的公寓，两间房，走廊和水龙头仍与隔壁共用。Josef Hofer 2帮着把床架抬上三楼，墙上的弹孔用石灰草草抹过。
  _effects: residence; cash -10 (deposit and haulage); mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; role_

- **1952.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara为圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsame出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113-evt:world-vienna-1912:011618-1946 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015000000000000013_

- **1952.09** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Luise Kral却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Klara把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1952.09** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Luise Kral敲了门。Klara掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Luise Kral什么也没说就走了。夜里Klara听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  铅笔头短得捏不住，Klara把它夹在两指之间，在表格背面写下两个名字。被漏掉的人一个叫扬，一个叫弗兰茨，都是每天清早从火车站走来的临时工。工头看了一眼背面，没说话，把表贴回墙上。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

- **1953.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Klara没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.03 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013076_

- **1953.01** `r15-wish.power-offer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/real-appointment` tags: politics, power, wish, office, status, vienna, urban, postwar
  Franz Winter把一份任命书推到Klara面前。职位不大，但有印章、有预算、有能签字的地方。Klara签下名字时，第一次觉得那件一直想着的事有了一个可以摸到的边。
  _effects: reputation 0.12000000000000005; position 部门主管 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:014422_

- **1953.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction

  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016458_

  _effects: project; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.02** `r3-war.dp-camp::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::camp-closure/reunited-with-neighbor` tags: war, displaced_persons, camp, postwar, bureaucracy, vienna, urban, reform
  Klara在营地门口等到一辆卡车，车上下来的人里有失散多年的Anna Bauer 3。两人隔着铁丝网看了半天，谁也不敢先叫名字；Luise Moser催着登记，Grete Stein替他们填了同一张表。那天夜里，帐篷里多了一个铺位。
  _effects: mood 0.14; flag +reunited_with_neighbor_at_dp_camp; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; knowledge “the neighbor survived and came to the same camp”_

- **1953.05** `vienna-social-movement.aftermath/burn-the-list` tags: development, social_movement, organization, consequence
  Klara在厨房的炉子里烧掉名单。纸灰卷起来，像黑色的叶子。Klara仍然去上工，但从那以后不再在公开场合提起圣马克斯殡葬合作社。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.10** `df18-blessing.laundry-water::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sit-with-the-stain` tags: blessing, omen, water, faith, fraud, consolation, religious, eerie
  洗衣房的墙上有一块水渍，形状像一个披着斗篷的女人。Klara第一次看见时，正好腰疼得直不起来；她点了一根蜡烛，坐了十分钟，疼没有消失，但呼吸慢了下来。Anton Winter说那是圣母，Klara没有纠正，也没有把水渍擦掉。
  _effects: cash -1 (洗衣房里的一根蜡烛); health 0.010000000000000009 (坐着休息了一会儿); mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “洗衣房的水渍像一个披斗篷的女人，我在它面前坐了一会儿。”; flag +df18_water_stain_consolation_

- **1954.01** `project_progressed` tags: project

  Klara把Maria Lechner 2的名字写在编制表的空行上，又在旁边注了“试用三个月”。Luise Moser提醒说预算只够半个位置，Klara还是把名字留下了。第二天，Maria Lechner 2提前半小时到，把走廊里的旧档案按年份重新排了一遍。
  _effects: position 助理文书 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:014422; organization_funds; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-149; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +hired_protege; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-149_

- **1954.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-ambulance` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  助理医生拿不准，叫了救护车。Klara被抬进医院，走廊里等了六个小时才排到床位；家里人后来算了一笔账，救护车比药贵。救护车在石板上颠，他抓着担架边，数着路灯。医院门口的担架已经排到街上。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; cash -15 (ambulance and hospital admission); health 0.04999999999999999 (admitted for observation and oxygen); role; knowledge “实习医生拿不准就会叫救护车；走廊等六个小时才有床，车费比药贵。”_

- **1954.04** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/signed-with-margin` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Klara在预算表上签了字，把冬季煤房的款项从三笔并成一笔，又给夜班守卫加了半份津贴。Anton Moser算出省下的钱刚好够修屋顶；没有人鼓掌，但一月的会议室不再需要穿大衣。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.040000000000000036; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

- **1954.06** `r1-fam.neighbor-noise::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/noise.endured` tags: social, neighbor, noise, water, dispute, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara没有吵，量了Alois Lechner 3敲锤子的时辰，发现他总在凌晨两点停手。Klara买了两团棉花，给自家每人分了一副；三个月后，Alois Lechner 3的手坏了，不再敲了。楼里安静下来，Klara反倒睡不着，半夜醒来听着那片空。
  _effects: cash -1 (半夜的锤子); condition; flag +learned_to_live_with_the_hammer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142_

- **1954.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Klara在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Emil Winter从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.04999999999999999 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000440:child_

  _effects: skill; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1928.04** `statistical_infection`

- **1928.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`

- **1928.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Franz Kral在楼梯间坐了很久；Mitzi在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Mitzi的名字；此后邻居提起Mitzi，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。

- **1938.02** `bc18-crime.inept-blackmail::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/photographer-sells-print` tags: crime, blackmail, photograph, mistake, humiliation, absurd, reputation, vienna
  Klara把那张照片卖给了《晨报》，配文写“送别者的疲惫”。Mitzi收到两克朗模特费和一份报纸；照片里的Mitzi歪着头，嘴半张，确实像哭过。邻居开始叫Mitzi“那个在葬礼上睡着的人”，语气不算尊敬，也不完全是嘲笑。
  _effects: cash 2 (报纸给的照片使用费); reputation 0.020000000000000018; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:371; flag +funeral_photo_in_the_paper; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.12** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Franz Winter点了牛奶咖啡，Mitzi要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1938.12** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-cart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  车夫把价钱咬得很死，Mitzi还到五克朗又添了二十赫勒，才说定跑一趟。Anton Weiss扶着床架，车过路口时煤箱歪了一下，细灰撒在路面上。到了地方，东西卸在门厅，天已经大亮。
  _effects: cash -6 (雇马车搬家具一趟的工钱); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1939.01** `bc18-fame.merchandise::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/carnival-stall` tags: fame, merchandise, business, absurd, legal, public, reputation, vienna
  厂里剩下一箱卖不掉的假胡子，Mitzi用三克朗买下，在狂欢节门口摆了个摊。孩子们把胡子别在帽檐上，年轻人买去参加化装舞会；一个老妇人买了两副，说给远行的儿子留着。收摊时，Mitzi数出五克朗，比本金多了两克朗，还剩下半箱胡子。
  _effects: cash 3 (狂欢节卖假胡子的利润); skill; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 22 -> 20; role; flag +sold_moustaches_at_carnival; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-audience-gets-bored` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  观众开始厌倦同一个故事。园艺会请了更年轻的发明家，女子学校把Mitzi的名字从海报上撤下；Mitzi在咖啡馆听见有人说“那个人还在讲火柴”。同一个月，一张报纸把旧照片配上新标题，问Mitzi是否已经“过气”。纠正的冲动又回来，但没有人再提问。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 15 -> 7; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.boredom 8 -> 20; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 0 -> 0; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Mitzi的名字从闲谈里慢慢退了下去。街上有了新的婚礼、新的葬礼和新的丑闻；只有裁缝还记得Mitzi改过两次同一件外套的腰身。
  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 30 -> 25; state_parameter known-affair.grief 5 -> 2; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/dismissed-as-nerves` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi把夜里咳醒的次数写在一张包装纸上，Elisabeth Weiss扫了一眼，说是心事太重，给了几包安神粉。走廊尽头的护士压低声音，说住院部还有空床，但要医生签字。Mitzi把包装纸折回去，没再问。
  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

- **1939.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/burn-the-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  等到第九天，Mitzi把没寄出的草稿和那封写了一半的信一起塞进炉子。纸卷起来，火苗发蓝，屋里一股焦味；Mitzi拿火钩把灰捅散，决定不再去问信的下落。炉门上的铁片烫手，那个“亲”字先没了。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +gave_up_on_letter; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1939.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Franz Stein 4在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Franz Stein 4敲开Mitzi的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1939.06** `ml18-business.bottle-cap-patent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/sell-the-patent-outright` tags: business, patent, money, windfall, invention, comic, vienna, urban
  破产拍卖会上，Mitzi用四克朗买下一张没人举牌的瓶盖专利，只因为图纸画得好看。三个月后，街角诊所的矿泉水厂找上门：那圈软木垫正好能解决他们的漏气问题。Elisabeth Berg核对完专利号，街角诊所当场开出八十克朗的支票。
  _effects: cash 80 (sale of the bottle-cap patent); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:403; reputation 0.06; flag +sold_patent; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.01** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/pay-and-regularize` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Berg把黑皮本翻到Mitzi那一页，说邮局储蓄的利息没有报，数目不大，但漏了就是漏了。Luise Kral 2在旁边把计算尺推来推去，最后算出一个八克朗的数目。Mitzi当天去缴了款，把收据贴在账本最后一页；回家的路上，账本第一次没有在口袋里折着。
  _effects: cash -8 (back taxes and a small fine on the windfall's interest); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 70 -> 62; reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; flag +taxes_paid; knowledge “邮局储蓄的利息也要报税，漏报的八克朗已经补齐。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Mitzi对Anton Weiss说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Anton Weiss把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Mitzi的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

  工会后院的墙根还堆着旧木牌，Mitzi 把炉子支在避风处，Hermann Winter 3 蹲着吹火，烟从墙头翻出去。二十只碗排在地上，汤盛到第七碗时锅底已经刮得响。
  _effects: cash -3 (买柴火和盐); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1940.10** `ml18-luck.strongbox-in-the-wall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-inflation-ate-them` tags: money, luck, housing, windfall, inflation, ownership, vienna, urban
  铁盒里的纸币已经作废，银扣也只值几个铜板。Hermann Berg 2把纸币一张张摊在窗台上，说战前的纸现在只能糊墙；Franz Winter笑得直不起腰，说这盒子在他家墙里藏了二十年，原来是一堆废纸。Mitzi把当票收好，去当铺换回一只银勺，勺柄上刻着外祖母的姓。
  _effects: cash 2 (the silver spoon redeemed with the old pawn ticket); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; flag +worthless_strongbox; knowledge “墙里的旧纸币已经作废，当票还能换回一只银勺。”; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1941.01** `r15-love.remarriage-after-scandal::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/registry-refusal` tags: love, marriage, scandal, family, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban, crisis
  登记员翻到旧报纸上的一条消息，说Mitzi的上一段关系“尚未了结”。Mitzi解释了半天，文件还是被推回来；Klara在门外等着，手里那束花慢慢垂了下去。
  _effects: cash -1 (fees for a refused registration); state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 20 -> 25; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +banns_refused_

- **1941.01** `r13-fam.care-sick-child::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::child-care-war/hospital-turned-away` tags: task, care, health, child, fever, night, vienna, urban
  Mitzi抱着Hermann Winter去医院，走廊里躺满缠绷带的士兵。挂号的人看了一眼孩子的脸，说病床先给伤员。Mitzi在门口站到天黑，最后用外套把孩子裹紧，走回家去；路上孩子问，医院为什么不收小孩。
  _effects: cash -1 (第三遍换湿布); flag +hospital_refused_child; knowledge “the hospital reserves beds for wounded soldiers”; condition_

  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Mitzi把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  Mitzi花光了积蓄，从战场运回一口薄棺。棺材到站时角上磕掉一块漆，Franz Winter说路上难免；开棺看了一眼，Mitzi就让盖上，再没有打开过。
  _effects: cash -24 (棺材铺的尺寸量错了); mood -0.06999999999999995; condition; flag +paid_to_bring_a_body_home; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; knowledge “the coffin arrived with a corner of paint knocked off”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

  Mitzi认定是电车售票员把纸包收进了自己的口袋，在站台上拦住他，声音大得整条街都回头。售票员把票夹和口袋都翻出来，里面只有几张旧票和一只坏表。Anton Lechner站在后面，把脸埋进围巾里。
  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:512; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; reputation -0.02999999999999997; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +accused_the_tram_conductor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; flag +something_is_missing_

  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Franz Moser攥着Mitzi的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1942.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital::pharmacy-war-requisition/pharmacy-war-flour-powder` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, crisis
  药房的后门开着，一个穿军大衣的人按包卖药：退烧粉、止血粉、止痛粉。Mitzi买了三包，回家发现其中一包是面粉；再去找人，后门已经锁了。他把那包面粉倒进碗里，又倒回纸袋，没舍得扔。
  _effects: cash -15 (three packets bought from a man in an army coat); health -0.020000000000000004 (one packet was flour, not medicine); condition; role; knowledge “后门按包卖的药可能是面粉；再回去找人，门已经锁了。”_

- **1942.06** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Mitzi走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.009999999999999995 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000584:child_

- **1942.08** `fa17-cabaret.debut::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/held-the-room` tags: art, performance, cabaret, debut, reputation, failure, theatre, vienna
  首演夜，Mitzi在第二首歌的中间忘了两句词，干脆停下来听乐队多走了一个小节，再接着唱。台下有人以为这是新加的停顿，笑了一声，随后整间屋子都放松了。Katharina Berg在后台说，今晚之后，节目单上可以印Mitzi的名字。
  _effects: state_add fame state:world-vienna-1912:513; cash 2 (首演酬劳与加演分成); role; reputation 0.06; skill; flag +debut_success; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

- **1943.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009702_

  半年过去，Mitzi又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 5 -> 11; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 55 -> 53; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1943.01** `kernel-family.housing-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/housing-relative.lodged-elsewhere` tags: task, task:family, family, housing, relatives, godparent, guardian, household
  Mitzi在楼里替教父Katharina Kral问了一圈，最后在洗衣妇家找到一张空床，每月三克朗。Katharina Kral把箱子搬过去，Mitzi替他垫了第一个月的房租；两人在楼梯口分开，Katharina Kral说等找到工就还。床板短了一截，箱子只能竖着放。
  _effects: cash -3 (first month’s bed rent for the relative); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; role; role; flag +relative_lodged_elsewhere; knowledge “洗衣妇家有一张空床，每月三克朗。”_

- **1943.07** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1943.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1943.11** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/mother-opened-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  回信没有到Mitzi手里。Elisabeth Hofer的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Elisabeth Weiss隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Elisabeth Hofer再见到Mitzi时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Mitzi站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。
  _effects: reputation -0.06_

- **1943.11** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/read-and-decline` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Mitzi把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1943.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1944.01** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1944.01** `sm18-object.child-schoolbag::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/searched-in-front-of-the-class` tags: child, school, object, accusation, misunderstanding, reputation, vienna, urban
  缝纫课上，Grete Stein让每个孩子把口袋翻出来。那只银顶针从Anton Lechner的书包夹层里滚到桌上，教室里一下安静了。Anton Lechner说是捡的，Grete Stein看着Mitzi，等一个大人拿主意。
  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:582; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003912:child; reputation -0.02999999999999997; flag +child_accused_of_taking_the_thimble; flag -something_is_missing; flag +something_is_missing_

  两个人一起出现在集市口。Mitzi讲预算，盟友讲谁家的屋顶漏了；散场时，一个卖苹果的老妇人塞给盟友一只苹果，说“你比报纸上看着像人”。Mitzi把这句话记了很久。

  Rosa Kral用红色油墨印了三十张海报，Mitzi把它们贴在面包房、澡堂和电车站的柱子上。海报上写着“新到咖啡，一磅九赫勒”，字歪了一点，价格却看得清。第三天，三个从没进过店的客人拿着海报来问，Mitzi把海报上的价又让了半赫勒。
  _effects: cash 5 (橱窗里的手写海报); reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +work13_poster_brought_customers; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

- **1945.01** `sm18-object.tram-lost::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/accuses-the-conductor` tags: object, loss, child, tram, family, everyday, vienna, urban
  Mitzi认定是电车售票员把纸包收进了自己的口袋，在站台上拦住他，声音大得整条街都回头。售票员把票夹和口袋都翻出来，里面只有几张旧票和一只坏表。Josef Moser站在后面，把脸埋进围巾里。

  Mitzi咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Mitzi手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Helene 3等在门口，问值不值，Mitzi只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.020000000000000018; skill_

  Mitzi把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Mitzi的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart)_

  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.010000000000000009_

### 212 subject events

- **1917.03** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  煤车刚过去，路心还留着几块碎煤。Josef的铁环撞上去，弹到水沟边。Mitzi Lechner先一步把铁环踩住，说要拿一颗石弹子来换。Josef蹲下去掏口袋，膝盖在石板棱上蹭掉一块皮，换回铁环时裤腿已经渗出血点；Josef没再争，只记住Mitzi Lechner踩铁环的那只脚。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.04; condition_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Josef站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Franz Kral睡醒哭了一声，Josef把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Josef Bauer回来时，Josef正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.019999999999999997; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000292:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1919.01** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场里的菜摊一字排开，Josef把十五克鲁泽排在掌心，先买了土豆，又去盐摊上要了一小包。摊主用旧报纸把盐裹好，Josef把它塞进围裙内袋，土豆兜在身前。回家时Katharina Bauer接过铜板一枚枚数，土豆一颗没少。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Josefine Hofer朝Josef招手。Josef挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Josef把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

  床单太长，Josef拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Ferdinand Winter弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Josef接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

  巷口有人跑过来，肩膀撞在Josef的胳膊上。烟画卡脱手，被风卷到路中间。Josef追了两步，鞋底在湿石板上打滑，手掌先着地。Mitzi从后面追上去，在拐角抓住那男孩的袖子，布条撕下来一截，人还是跑了。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; condition_

  Josef走到第四步，鞋底在霜上滑了一下，整个人挂在栏杆外侧。Mitzi抓住Josef的手腕，Klara跑去叫桥头卖烤栗子的老人；Josef被拉上来时，膝盖已经湿透，第二天走路一瘸一拐。

  Josef在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Klara。这一次对方先点了头，Josef准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Josef才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 48 -> 53; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Mitzi Kral把算术本摊在厨房桌上，Josef用豆子摆出十以内的加减。Mitzi Kral算错时，Josef不骂，只把豆子重新排一遍。两个月后，Mitzi Kral的听写和算术都及格了，Josef Bauer在饭桌上多给Josef盛了半碗汤。
  _effects: flag +tutored_sibling_well; skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000427:child_

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 56 -> 48; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.secret true -> true; flag +yf18_gave_tickets_away; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Josef把蒙眼布扯下来，说这种誓词不背。地下室里安静了一瞬，Elisabeth Winter把酒杯放下，说走出去就别回来。Josef走到楼梯口，听见身后有人把杯子摔在地上。第二天，宿舍门口用粉笔画了一只鸡，旁边写着Josef的名字。

  Josef在工厂后街租了一个床位，和两个车工合住。房东太太每天收一次煤钱，洗脸水要自己下楼打。第一夜，他听着隔壁的机器声睡着，梦里还在数工票上的尺寸。天没亮，隔壁的车工就敲门叫他一起上工。
  _effects: cash -3 (行李放在楼梯口); mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +moved_to_lodging_near_work; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; move place-wien-printshop-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne只招日工。Josef天亮前到门口，名字写在临时名单上，干一天算一天，一天一克朗，月底结二十六克朗。工头说下个月可能有正式位置，也可能没有；Josef把每天的工时记在烟盒背面，怕漏掉一天。

  Josef和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Josef在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Josef不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Josef拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Josef的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); health -0.12 (injury and lost job); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Anton把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Josef留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Josef在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods); mood -0.05999999999999994; role_

- **1932.01** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-water-goes-cold` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, reform
  Klara坐在床边，Josef用搪瓷盆替她洗头。水凉了又添，肥皂泡顺着耳后流下来；Klara闭着眼说，小时候她母亲也是这样替她洗的。窗外有人收煤，铁锹刮过石板，两个人谁也没有动。

  Josef在诊所走廊的第三张长凳上坐到中午，膝上压着挂号纸和一块湿手帕。Alois Berg把听诊器贴上来不到半分钟，说是潮气和烟，开了褐色止咳糖浆，又让下周再来。药费从当天的菜钱里扣走，回家时篮子里只剩半棵卷心菜。
  _effects: cash -3 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); health -0.030000000000000027 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); condition; flag +clinic_first_visit; knowledge “the clinic doctor called the cough chronic bronchitis”_

  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Elisabeth Weiss的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。

  货场的煤烟落在肩上。Klara帮他扛第一袋时教他把腰沉下去。站务员在名册边上记了日期，说干得好才有正式名分，Josef点头，把袋子往肩上一顶。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ morgue:person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000584:child-evt:world-vienna-1912:005938; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1937.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Josef看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

- **1937.05** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Katharina Bauer没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1937.06** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-picked-coal` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock, injury
  同来的Emil Weiss 2没被点上，骂了一声把麻绳甩上肩走了。Josef跟着工头进了货栈，煤块从跳板滑进船舱，到午后点了四克朗二十赫勒，手指黑得洗不净。
  _effects: cash 4.2 (卸煤半日的工钱); health -0.050000000000000044 (扛煤筐磨破肩膀); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

- **1937.07** `r3-crime.crime-scene::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::glass-and-blood/kept-the-crowd-back` tags: crime, police, evidence, street, mistake, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef把围观的人挡在粉笔线外，又用一只木箱扣住那半枚鞋印。Franz Moser来的时候，先看了看地上的血，再看了看Josef，说“你倒懂得不少”。Josefine Hofer在人群里喊“我家孩子还没回来”，Anton Stein 2让她先登记。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +preserved_the_crime_scene; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; knowledge “the footprint was covered before the crowd arrived”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-depression/short-time` tags: adulthood, depression, work, injury
  Josef改成每周上三天班，工钱按日结算。家里的面包从每天一条变成隔天一条，Josef开始把午饭省下来带回家。
  _effects: cash -3 (short-time work); health -0.040000000000000036 (reduced food); condition_

  _effects: state_remove oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:309; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice_

- **1938.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.join-second-row` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Josef把两克朗月费和徽章钱一起放在啤酒渍斑斑的桌上，Emil Weiss 3在名册上添了一行。钢琴缺的那个音在排练中途又卡住，Josef站在第二排跟着哼，声音被两边的人盖住，胸腔里却震得发麻；散场时能把副歌完整唱下来，也记住了怎么在人群里找准自己的声部。
  _effects: cash -2 (合唱团月费和铜徽章钱); mood 0.07000000000000006; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1938.04** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.closed` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard, injury
  Josef把窗关上，争吵声立刻变成闷响，像隔着一层棉被。可那男人的一句话还是从缝里钻进来，她躺到床上翻来覆去，数着墙上的水渍，到很晚才睡着。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (被楼下争吵搅得没睡好); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1938.08** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef请楼下的Karl Kraus白天来照看Katharina Bauer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Karl Kraus上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Josef下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Katharina Bauer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱); food; health 0.04 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗)_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Franz Moser在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Josef排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1939.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Katharina Moser 2的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1939.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hand-over-the-grain` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Berg把征用单按在柜台上，Josef家里的两袋面粉被划走一袋，换回一张盖蓝章的收据。Elisabeth Berg说下周还会来，如果地窖里再翻出粮食，收据就不作数了。
  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价（其中4克朗记入债务）); food; flag +requisition_receipt; knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”_

- **1939.01** `p10-migration-tasks.find-lodging/lodging-no-guarantor` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, lodging, arrival
  看门人看了他的身份证，说三处房都要担保人签字，他一个也拿不出。房东把地址纸条还给他，他只好拖着箱子去车站候车室的长椅上过夜，半夜被巡夜人叫醒查了两次身份证。第二天他再去门房，看门人只摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; health -0.030000000000000027 (在硬木长椅上冻了一夜，腰背发僵); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1939.02** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.turned-back` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river, injury
  Josef走到半路风忽然大起来，围巾抽在脸上生疼，她掉头往回走。路过面包店时橱窗里还剩几个昨天的圆面包，四赫勒一个，她买了两个，揣在怀里带回去当晚饭。
  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个（其中0.08克朗记入债务）); health -0.010000000000000009 (在风口里走了一段，脸被吹得发红)_

- **1940.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Katharina Moser 2没有答应，只说现在不合适。Josef把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007747_

  Mitzi Kral在城里站稳了脚，托人带来一个信封，里面是八克朗和一张夜校报名单。信上只写：当年你教我算术，现在轮到我付学费。Josef把报名单看了三遍，填好，第二天一早就去交了。
  _effects: cash 8 (多年后的一封家信); flag +sibling_repaid_tutoring; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000427:child_

- **1940.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Friedrich Stein在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Friedrich Stein在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Josef把最后一批货折价卖给街口的贩子，招牌摘下来靠在门后。Josef Bauer坐在空柜台后面，说这铺子开了三十七年，第一次白天上门板。Josef没有接话，只把钥匙放在Josef Bauer手边；两人听见街上的失业队伍从门口走过去。
  _effects: cash 4 (把家里的招牌重新钉上); condition; flag +closed_family_shop; role; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “the shop traded for thirty-seven years before closing”_

  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Josef用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Grete Weiss 2指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

  Josef把通知念给Katharina Moser听，Katharina Moser摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Josef去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008803_

- **1941.01** `r14-soc.confront-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/brought-a-witness` tags: task, rumor, reputation, neighbor_conflict, confrontation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef把门房的老婆子请到院子里，请对方说那晚Josef一直在洗衣房。Klara听完，说自己也是听来的。话没有完全断，但至少不再从Klara嘴里出来。门房的老婆子临走前说“下回别为这种事敲我的门”。后来逢人便说，那晚是门房的老婆子替Josef作保，Josef欠下一个人情。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; reputation 0.05; flag +brought_witness_to_rumor; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the porter wife can confirm the subject alibi”_

- **1941.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/keys-and-photo-returned` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Josef把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Friedrich Stein把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; mood -0.08000000000000018; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1941.07** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Grete Stein 2把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Josef把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Grete Stein 2低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.020000000000000018 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase)_

- **1941.11** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Franz Lechner在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.12** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.caretaker-notice` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef去找看门人，说院子里四面墙，声音绕不出去。第二天楼梯间的木板上贴了一张纸：晚上十点后请勿放留声机，鞋匠铺也请收锤。纸条没有落款，只有房东事务所的戳。Grete Stein路过时看了很久，把留声机的盖子合上了。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

  两名工人把Josefine Hofer家的钢琴抬上卡车，琴腿在门框上磕掉一块漆。Elisabeth Berg把一张纸塞给Josef，说只要签“屋内无人认领”，就不必再搬第二趟。Josef签了名，看见Josefine Hofer站在对面窗帘后，手一直按在玻璃上。

  街上的人都在排队领救济，铺子里一天没有进来一个客人。Josef把面粉分成小包，赊给楼里的邻居，账记在门板背面。Josef Bauer说这样下去撑不过冬天；Josef把最后一袋盐倒进罐子，说撑一天算一天。
  _effects: cash -8 (把家里的招牌重新钉上); food; condition; flag +kept_shop_open_during_crisis; role; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “the neighbours owe the shop more than they can pay”; employment Kaufmann @ cinema:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5-evt:world-vienna-1912:003632_

  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Josef胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.030000000000000027 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙)_

  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +parent_hospitalized_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; role; flag +marriage_blocked_by_lodging; cash -1 (bread from the bakery); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

  Josef忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Josef听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。

  Josef替Elisabeth Weiss写信，把最后知道的部队番号、收容站和一处旧住址都写上去。Elisabeth Weiss不识字，却要Josef把“家里都好”念三遍，怕写错。信投进邮筒时，她伸手在筒口摸了一下；Josef把回信地址又描粗了一笔。
  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); flag +wrote_to_missing_relative; skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; knowledge “A letter with the last known unit and shelter addresses was ”_

- **1944.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction

- **1950.07** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/broken-tooth` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  夜里十一点，母狮不肯碰桶里的马肉。Josef举着煤油灯绕到兽栏后面，看见它右槽牙断了一截，牙龈肿得发亮。Josef把肉切碎，又去敲Josef Winter的门；第二天兽医来了，Anton Moser在晨会上第一次记住Josef的名字。
  _effects: organization_found 普拉特动物园夜班队 (other); position 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:014420; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:765; skill; reputation 0.029999999999999992; flag +zoo_night_shift; knowledge “母狮的右槽牙断了，牙龈肿得发亮，夜里不肯吃马肉。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1950.11** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef数出八赫勒交给Grete Stein，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Grete Stein说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Josef盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1951.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, injury
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Josef一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Gustav Winter送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); health -0.030000000000000027 (crowded sleeping space); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000740:child_

- **1951.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-seal-was-used` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Josef Winter用Josef留在抽屉里的印章签了一份采购单，把库房里最后一批煤卖给了城外的砖厂。Josef发现时，账已经做平，签字栏里是Josef自己的名字。Josef Winter说只是替Josef省了麻烦，还把那支用旧的印泥放回了原处。
  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱); reputation -0.1; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand; mood -0.019999999999999907_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 14; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1951.07** `r2-law.savings-passbook::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/clerk-covers-shortfall` tags: finance, bank, savings, household, contract, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Winter把利息算错了一格，少给Josef两克朗。Josef指出来，Josef Winter翻了三遍利率表，最后从自己口袋补上。Franz Moser正好来查柜，问这笔钱为什么不在账上；Josef Winter说是“上月的尾差”，Franz Moser把这句话记进本子。
  _effects: cash 2 (存折上的蓝格子); flag +caught_interest_shortfall; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; knowledge “the clerk paid the interest shortfall from his own pocket”_

  Josef Winter在签字前把一张剧院包厢的票根留在桌上，说是“给孩子的”。Josef把票根推回去，却在许可证上签了名；排水图仍缺一页，但窗口当天就盖了章。扩建开工后，第一场雨把后院淹了半尺。
  _effects: flag +permit_for_favor; knowledge “许可证少了一页排水图，盖章时没有补。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: reputation -0.03_

- **1952.09** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Luise Moser敲了门。Josef掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Luise Moser什么也没说就走了。夜里Josef听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013808_

- **1953.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Josef和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Josef把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:855; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156; flag +office_under_siege_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。

- **1953.07** `oj18-zoo.escape::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/chimp-on-the-prater` tags: zoo, animals, escape, night, blame, work, odd_job, vienna
  黑猩猩“卡斯帕”用一根弯钉挑开第二道门，顺着排水沟跑进普拉特。Josef提着一桶苹果追到旋转木马后面，看见它坐在售票亭顶上，正把一张张票根往嘴里塞。Josef Winter赶来时，Josef已经用苹果把它引进了送货马车。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 14 -> 22; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 20 -> 25; cash -1 (apples used to lure the chimpanzee); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; flag +monkey_returned; knowledge “卡斯帕会用弯钉挑门闩，苹果比网子管用。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155_

- **1953.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Josef当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1953.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.opened` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  信封没封牢，Josef在灯下把它掀开，里面是维也纳联合银行的催款单，欠了三十四克朗。她认出那名字是楼上常穿黑裙的女人，便把纸原样叠回去，用米汤把封口粘好，放到门房窗台上。
  _effects: flag +knows_upstairs_debt_

- **1954.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/auditor-is-the-beneficiary` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  审计开始前，Luise Kral把名片放在桌上：他是Anna Berg 2的弟弟。Josef看着他翻开哥哥的考勤卡，又合上，说家里的事不影响审计。抽查只做了半天，报告只有一页，最后一页的签名是空的。
  _effects: flag +auditor_was_beneficiary; knowledge “审计员的哥哥就是去年那个顾问，报告最后一页没有签名。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  第二天的报纸又登了一封匿名信，说Josef把一份合同给了亲戚。信里没有证据，但足够让两个原本答应到场的人临时出差。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 40; state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 54; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Anna Berg 2用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Josef才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156; flag +parish_registered_

- **1955.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/motion-fails` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  会议室里有人念了一份动议，说Josef把预算当成人情。Franz Winter把签名页传了一圈，最后停在自己手里：支持票比反对票少三张。Josef没有发言，只把下一季度的支出表放在桌上，表上每一笔都有收据。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; knowledge “动议只拿到少数支持，三名原本签名的委员在投票前撤回了。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1955.01** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef没有立刻答话，先去敲Hermann Weiss的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Hermann Weiss提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Josef把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1955.01** `state_expire:office-under-siege` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  Josef的「职位受围」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:855; flag -office_under_siege_

- **1955.01** `r13-fam.vouch-for-friend::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/friend-defaults` tags: task, social, friendship, debt, guarantee, risk, vienna, urban
  到了季末，Hermann Weiss没有还钱，人也搬走了。Anna Berg 2拿着借据找到Josef的门口，说第二个名字不是白签的。Josef把家里能卖的铜壶和旧表都拿出来，还是差一截；Anna Berg 2坐在门槛上，说下个月再来。
  _effects: cash -14 (借据上的第二个名字（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +friend_defaulted_on_guaranteed_loan; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155; knowledge “the friend left the district without repaying the loan”_

- **1955.02** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-part-cash-part-furniture` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你数出四十赫勒搁在秤盘上，说剩下的一克朗用那张旧折叠桌抵。老板撇了撇嘴，让伙计跟你上楼搬桌子。桌子腿有点晃，但木料还结实。邻居在楼道里搭了把手，账本上这一页只写了一行，没写利息。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买煤付的部分现钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156_

- **1955.10** `ml18-investment.transylvanian-mine::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-mine-was-salted` tags: investment, mining, fraud, money, risk, business, vienna, urban
  抽水机运到山里才发现，矿洞的岩壁上早被人撒过金粉，岩芯也是挑过的。Josef Winter在两个月前把股份转给了一家空壳公司，办公室只剩一张桌子和半瓶墨水。Grete Huber 2把调查报告放在Josef面前，最后一页写着“建议不予起诉”，因为找不到被告。
  _effects: cash -50 (the investment in a salted mine（其中46.4克朗记入债务）); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:936; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; condition; flag +salted_mine; knowledge “矿洞岩壁上的金粉是撒上去的，岩芯也被挑过。”; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +fortune_reversed; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1956.01** `ml18-reversal.old-partner-returns::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/he-was-the-scammer` tags: reversal, money, betrayal, business, scam, comeback, vienna, urban
  Anna Berg 2喝到第三杯才承认：当年那批糖厂债券是他自己印的，受害者不止Josef一个。他从皮包里拿出一张名单和三十克朗，说这是六个人凑出来的退款，剩下的大概追不回来了。Josef把名单折好，没有问那三十克朗里有没有Anna Berg 2自己的钱。
  _effects: cash 30 (partial refund from the old partner's other victims); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:978; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156; flag +old_partner_confessed; knowledge “旧合伙人承认糖厂债券是他自己印的，受害者至少有六个人。”; mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.019999999999999997; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1956.01** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction

  补洞的钱又从另一个洞漏出去：煤价涨了，房东换了锁，那个说好下月还钱的人搬走了。Josef在账本上把同一笔支出记了两次，第二次是因为不相信第一次。夜里听见楼梯响，就以为是债主。

  链子从你指缝里滑下去，落在绒布上几乎没有声音。掌柜说两克朗四十赫勒，你点了点头。出门时你把表掏出来上了几圈弦，它还在走，只是再没法挂在马甲上了。

  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000004; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:1021; flag +found_cooked_ledger; knowledge “前任账本里两页收据号码连续，却来自两家不同商号。”; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:ml18-reversal/reversal-half-year/another-turn-of-the-screw` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  补洞的钱又从另一个洞漏出去：煤价涨了，房东换了锁，那个说好下月还钱的人搬走了。Josef在账本上把同一笔支出记了两次，第二次是因为不相信第一次。夜里听见楼梯响，就以为是债主。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -53 -> -61; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 58 -> 66; cash -2 (coal, a new lock and a missing debtor)_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Josef想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.04** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-refused` tags: faith, social, family, home

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155; reputation -0.04000000000000001_

- **1957.05** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/hold-list-and-watch` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hermann Weiss把会费账本推回去，说外头那个组织答应给煤，只要名单。Josef把账本合上，用一块旧布包好塞进缝纫机底下，说先看看谁在往外递话。两人都没再提这事。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155; flag +kept_district_list_private_

- **1957.08** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Josef去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为普拉特动物园夜班队的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156_
## Rosa

Born 1912.01, died 1922.06 (age 10).
Education: none 0; occupation: none; roles: infant, child.

### State snapshot
No mechanism state was ever attached.

### 15 subject events

- **1913.07** `vienna-life-infant-feeding/fed-enough` tags: infancy, food, household
  Rosa Lechner在Lechner-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt给Rosa喂奶，随后把洗净的布尿片挂在窗边。孩子吃饱后睡着了，锅里还留着半碗燕麦糊。
  _effects: cash -2 (milk and oatmeal)_

- **1914.04** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Lechner-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1914.06** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dead-grandmothers-name` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Rosa扶着桌沿站起来，指着门口的空处，清清楚楚叫出一个名字。屋里没人教过这个词；Rosa Lechner手里的铝勺停在半空。那是去年冬天埋掉的老人的名字，窗外正好有送煤的车经过，铁轮压过石缝。
  _effects: mood -0.03; knowledge “孩子说出的第一个词，是去年死去的祖母的名字。”; flag +first_word_dead_name_

- **1914.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1914.07** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1916.10** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/wooden-horse` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Rosa Lechner把报纸包推到Rosa面前，包得紧，Rosa拆到第三层才看见木头。木马只有巴掌大，四条腿磨得圆滑，鬃毛是一刀一刀刻出来的。Rosa把它揣进衣襟，上楼时用手护着，晚上放在枕头边，袖口蹭了一道黑印。
  _effects: mood 0.08; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Rosa的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Rosa在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Luise Moser把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Rosa伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Luise Moser从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Rosa手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Rosa一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Rosa后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Rosa没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Rosa用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1921.01** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Rosa想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Josef Bauer听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Rosa试着站起来，被Josef Bauer按回台阶上。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Rosa站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Josef Stein在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Rosa掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Rosa才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1921.12** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Josef Bauer朝Rosa招手。Rosa挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Rosa把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1922.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1922.06** `statistical_death/pneumonia` tags: death
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang发起高烧，咳嗽越来越深。邻居帮忙去请医生，医生到时只留下药粉和一张账单；几天后呼吸变得很浅。Mitzi Lechner在场。
  _effects: death (肺炎)_

  Grete Stein从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Emil答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Emil回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Grete Stein看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1924.05** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/take-over-bed-and-ledger` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anna Bauer走后，靠墙那张床空了出来，Emil把自己的铺盖搬过去，也接过那本记着欠账的旧账本：面包店三克朗、煤铺一克朗八十赫勒。晚上Emil坐在床沿一笔笔抄清，第一次知道家里的钱是怎么缺的。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.05; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

- **1924.06** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Emil在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Emil用牙咬断，重新来。Katharina Bauer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Emil把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1924.06** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-sign-up-hike` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Emil把十赫勒放在桌上的铁皮盒里，翻到歌本最后一页，用铅笔写下自己的名字。Mitzi Lechner在后面戳Emil的背，说乡下的草地能躺着听云。
  _effects: cash -10 (青年会远足车费); relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.03_

- **1925.01** `r2-faith.parish-school-fee::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/expelled-for-arrears` tags: faith, school, charity, education, fees, reform, medical, schooling
  欠了兩個月学费，Alois Hofer把Emil的座位调到最后一排，说书本先留下。Helene Winter在门口拦住Emil，说学校不是救济院。Emil回家没有说，第二天照常出门，在火车站坐到放学时间。Katharina Bauer后来在书包里发现了一张空白的退学单。
  _effects: mood -0.14; condition; flag +expelled_from_parish_school_for_arrears; relation rival with person-helene-winter; knowledge “two months of unpaid fees can cost a child his seat”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Emil就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Emil还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Emil在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (考前最后一夜的煤油); mood 0.06; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1925.04** `r1-child.gymnasium-books::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sunday-latin-lessons` tags: gifted, gymnasium, books, class, teacher, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil考上了免费名额，可拉丁文跟不上。Grete Stein每周日来家里补课，用茶匙在桌上画词尾。Josef Bauer煮一壶淡茶，三个人围着一张摇腿的桌子，把一页课文读了一整个下午。
  _effects: flag +teacher_tutored_on_sundays; skill; relation friend with person-grete-stein; education secondary_

- **1925.08** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Josefine Hofer先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Emil每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.039999999999999994; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:48; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.12** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/let-friend-return-it` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Emil把手套交给Mitzi，让Mitzi替自己去还。Mitzi回来时说，Klara问是谁捡到的；Mitzi顺口报了一个名字，Emil听见那名字不是自己的，笑得比平时响。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:47; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.009999999999999995; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/printshop-apprentice` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Emil下周一到排字房报到，先搬铅字和扫地，月薪十八克朗。家里用第一笔预付工资买了双结实的鞋。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance)_

- **1926.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil在衣帽间堵住Josef，问那两张票怎么到了Klara手里。Josef说Emil从来没敢开口，自己只是替Emil把话说了；Emil把票根揉成一团，塞进Josef的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 35 -> 40; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 20; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 10 -> 5; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.02; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1926.01** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002340_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/talked-about` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Emil只把Klara的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Emil是不是要请人喝咖啡；Emil说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 20 -> 32; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 0; reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Emil又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 28; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  客户刚把床单接过去，Josef Winter就从楼梯口上来，盯着Emil看了两秒，说“你不是这家的”。Josef Winter当场把工钱收回，说明天要去找Klara的雇主。Emil站在门口，手里还攥着空筐绳，回家一路上没吃东西，Klara问起来，只摇了摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西)_

- **1926.07** `r3-teen.police-caution::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-parent-notice/fined` tags: police, caution, record, family, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Katharina Bauer说按条例罚三克朗，交不出就通知学校。Josef Bauer从内衣口袋里摸出叠好的钞票，一张张数，数到第三张时手停了。回家后，那三克朗被写在厨房的账单上，旁边画了一道红线。
  _effects: cash -3 (派出所里的第一次签字); mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +fined_by_police; relation rival with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1926.09** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/climb-back-down` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Mitzi骂了一句，Klara却把围巾解下来递给Emil；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 40 -> 43; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 5 -> 0; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Josef在第二支曲子前把Mitzi请走，说Emil答应过要替她看管外套。Emil抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Emil把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。

  Emil没有钱请老师，就趴在窗边听隔壁的留声机，再把听到的调子按在琴弦上。手指磨出茧子，音还是不准，但他第一次拉完了一整支曲子。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +learned_instrument_by_ear; skill_

  Emil趁Josef Bauer去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Emil趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Emil说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:88; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  Emil揣着止咳糖继续上工，排到下午手一抖，整行字歪进字夹。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne看见发青的嘴唇，让Emil去后院筛一星期的旧铅字——更脏，但不用赶时。
  _effects: health -0.06000000000000005 (带咳上工并筛旧铅字); skill; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

  补考那天，Emil在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Grete Stein跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Emil去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; education apprenticeship_

  _effects: health -0.17999999999999994 (hand caught in press); condition; cash -4 (hospital transport)_

  Grete Stein在班上念了Emil的作文，念到“她的手套放在木座上”时停了一下，说这就是考试里最危险的东西：把私事当成论据。全班笑了，Emil盯着桌面上的墨水圈，听见Klara在最后一排合上了书。

  Mitzi Kral把铅笔芯写断了两次，第三遍还是算错，眼泪滴在本子上，把墨迹洇开。Emil把本子拿开，说再写一遍，孩子却把椅子往后一推。母亲在灶边喊别吵，Emil只好把豆子收进碗里，听见Mitzi Kral在床上抽鼻子。

  检查员来查佣工合同，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Emil把手藏在围裙下面。Emil没有藏，摊开掌心，那块新疤还没有褪成白色。检查员看了一眼，又看了一眼Friedrich Winter，把名字写进本子。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说这是烫衣服不小心，谁家没有这种事。

  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.040000000000000036; health -0.040000000000000036 (连续夜间加班)_

  Emil每晚给Mitzi Kral讲题，自己的作业只能留到深夜。期中考试，Emil的名次掉了十几位，Grete Stein在家长会上点名。回家后，Emil看见Mitzi Kral趴在桌上睡着了，算术本还摊在胳膊下面，就没有叫醒。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +neglected_own_studies; relation rival with person-josef-bauer_

- **1929.01** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Emil把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Emil用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 59 -> 56; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 71 -> 73_
