- **1929.03** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/take-it-to-the-director` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil把油印纸交到校长室，Elisabeth Berg正好坐在里面喝茶。校长问纸是从哪儿来的；Emil说在课本里捡的。Maria Bauer当天下午被叫走，第二天换了一个代课老师；Emil的拉丁文成绩突然好了起来，教室里却没有人再和Emil说话。
  _effects: reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; flag +yf18_denounced_teacher; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1929.05** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/answer-in-secret` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, reform, medical, schooling
  格拉茨的表姐来信，说旅馆缺一个叠床单的女孩，管吃住，工钱少但能攒下。Emil把信藏在算术本里，用铅笔在背面写回信；邮票是从Josef Bauer的抽屉里偷的，信封上不敢写自己的名字，只写了“内详”。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:136; cash -1 (a stolen stamp and writing paper); flag +yf18_cousin_reply; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1929.11** `yf18-performance.choir-solo::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/laugh-and-bow` tags: performance, choir, humiliation, school, voice, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil听见自己的声音劈开，索性停下来，对着教堂的穹顶笑了一声。Maria Bauer在风琴后面瞪眼，Emil却把那句拉丁文降了八度重新唱了一遍，声音厚得像另一个人；台下有人先笑，随后跟着鼓掌，Luise Berg在最后一排把手套举起来挥了挥。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000060:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; flag +yf18_laughed_at_solo; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Elisabeth Lechner没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Emil回头看了一眼，Elisabeth Lechner正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

- **1930.01** `yf18-runaway.north-station::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/board-the-train` tags: runaway, station, train, papers, escape, reform, medical, schooling
  北站的钟指到十一点四十，Emil把包袱塞进头顶的行李架，票根攥在手心。Elisabeth Berg在检票口翻看Emil的工票，问去格拉茨做什么；Emil说去看表姐，声音没有抖。汽笛响时，Emil看见站台上没有一张熟悉的脸，忽然笑了一下。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:136; move place-wien; cash -4 (the fare and a platform coffee); flag +yf18_left_vienna; mood 0.050000000000000044; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1930.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Emil的「第一次心动」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:47; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1930.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.join-second-row` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Emil把两克朗月费和徽章钱一起放在啤酒渍斑斑的桌上，Anna Weiss在名册上添了一行。钢琴缺的那个音在排练中途又卡住，Emil站在第二排跟着哼，声音被两边的人盖住，胸腔里却震得发麻；散场时能把副歌完整唱下来，也记住了怎么在人群里找准自己的声部。
  _effects: cash -2 (合唱团月费和铜徽章钱); mood 0.07000000000000006; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1930.02** `p9-work-printshop.printed-secret/tell-foreman` tags: work, printshop, printing, compositor, craft, workshop, secret, handbill
  Emil把传单的事原样说给Friedrich Winter听，Friedrich Winter说照印，钱照收，但把原稿锁进自己的抽屉，钥匙揣在背心口袋里。Elisabeth Berg若来问，两人就说排的是戏单。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; cash 1.5 (传单活分到的工钱); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1930.04** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Emil把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Emil没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 5 -> 13; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 60 -> 58_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003512_

- **1930.10** `major-education-night-technical-school/night-school-certificate` tags: education, night_school, work, exhaustion, major
  夜校在第七区的旧校舍里，十二个工人挤在一张长桌前。Friedrich Moser用粉笔画出齿轮的剖面，又让大家传看一只烧坏的保险盒。Emil把图纸带回家，在厨房桌上描到半夜。结业考试通过后，Emil拿到一张机械制图的证书，学费一共九克朗。
  _effects: education secondary; skill; cash -9 (evening-school fees); role; flag +night_school_certificate; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1931.01** `vienna-adult-social.wage-loan/wage-loan.granted` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Emil在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的账房里向Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne开口预支十二克朗，因为家里的煤和面包钱同时到期。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne从抽屉里数出钞票，让会计在发薪日扣回，并记下“下不为例”。
  _effects: cash 12 (预支工资，先拿到现金); reputation 0.05_

- **1931.01** `p9-work-career.clerk-post/p9-work-career.clerk-post.hired` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, office, application
  Emil在保险公司写字间的小桌上抄完一页德文书信，又算出利息表上最后一行，速记只漏了两个词。Helene Kral把卷子翻过来看背面，说手是稳的。月底他领到七十克朗，扣掉十五克朗保证金，签字画押，坐进靠窗那把椅子，桌上有一块吸墨纸和一支借来的笔。
  _effects: cash 55 (首月工钱扣除保证金后实发); skill; employment 保险写字间试用办事员 @ org-winter-print; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1931.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  窗外有人来问入会，接待的人一时不知道怎么开口，Emil替他说“进来坐”。老成员把账本拿回去，用一块旧布包好，放进抽屉最里面。抽屉关上的声音比平时响。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +union_debt_written_off; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/dismissed-after-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, unemployment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Emil不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Emil拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Emil的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); health -0.12 (injury and lost job); cash -6 (medical treatment（其中3克朗记入债务）)_

- **1932.01** `oj18-radio.repair::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/cold-joint` tags: radio, repair, craft, customer, work, odd_job, suspense, reform
  Grete Huber的收音机只发出炒豆子一样的杂音。Emil把后板拆开，发现一根接地线被老鼠咬断，焊点冷得像一颗坏牙。修好以后，Grete Huber留Emil听完了整点新闻；出门时，Emil在巷口还听见那台机器在响。
  _effects: organization_found 屋顶无线电修理铺 (household_firm); position 无线电修理匠 @radio:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:004510; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:227; cash 2 (radio repair fee); skill; reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; flag +radio_repairer; knowledge “收音机的接地线被老鼠咬断，焊点冷得像一颗坏牙。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +wish:love:pursuing_

- **1932.01** `p10-migration-tasks.return-home/return-ticket-bought` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, return, train
  他在南站买了一张三等车票，十二克朗四十赫勒，把木工工具留给了同屋的工友。车过布吕恩时换了一次车，哥哥站在站台上，手里拎着一壶水。第二天一早他就跟着下地，手上磨出了新泡。
  _effects: cash -13 (回乡三等车票与路上买面包的钱（其中11克朗记入债务）); mood 0.07000000000000006; flag +worked_in_vienna_factory; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Emil在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Emil把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004509_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle
  Emil和Marie Huber在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Marie Huber把煤钱推过来，Emil接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-and-mother-weak` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  孩子出生后取名Otto Bauer，Marie Huber一直出血。Emil跑去医院，医生来晚了，只留下药和十八克朗的账单；Marie Huber落下产后虚弱的毛病，Emil抱着孩子坐在床边，直到邻居把汤送来，才发现自己的鞋穿反了。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.05999999999999974 (postpartum bleeding); cash -18 (doctor and medicine（其中18克朗记入债务）); condition; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:256_

- **1933.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Emil听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 18; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 27; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  两年过去，Emil已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Emil也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 27 -> 37; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 18 -> 28; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1933.01** `r13-fam.introduce-partner::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/accepted-at-table` tags: task, family, courtship, family_meal, partner, mother, reform, medical
  Emil把Marie Huber领进厨房，Katharina Bauer正在切面包，刀没有停。Marie Huber把带来的果酱放在桌角，说自家做的；Katharina Bauer看了一眼，让它搁着。吃饭时，Katharina Bauer问了三遍家里的情况，第三遍时语气松了；Marie Huber走时，Katharina Bauer把剩下的半条面包塞进了Marie Huber的口袋。
  _effects: cash -2 (星期天的第三副碗筷（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); flag +partner_accepted_at_family_table; role; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; knowledge “the mother sent half a loaf home with the partner”_

- **1933.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Marie Huber拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失)_

- **1933.06** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/accepts-the-file` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, reform
  户籍官Friedrich Hofer为了结掉一桩陈年失踪案，在档案上给Emil盖了“推定死亡”。三天后，Emil收到一张慰问卡和一本作废的配给簿；同一天，追了半年的债主忽然不再上门。旧货商听说后，花两克朗买走一份抄本，说要给一个躲债的朋友看看格式。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖给旧货商的死亡证明抄本); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:257; reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +declared_dead_while_alive; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1934.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Marie Huber抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Emil把床单对折，腾出一半，Marie Huber用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004974_

- **1934.01** `bc18-ghost.own-funeral::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/second-corpse` tags: bureaucracy, funeral, mistaken_identity, public, absurd, death, humiliation, vienna
  Luise Kral把另一具同名尸体推进后间，说档案上只有一个名字，不能开两场葬礼。两个家庭在门口争起来，Emil只好当场解开领口，让众人看脖子上的胎记。警察来了，记下姓名和住址；Katharina Moser 2在一旁小声问，那今晚到底埋谁。
  _effects: cash -2 (警察局的手续费和车钱); reputation -0.03; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 20 -> 40; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 1 -> 3; relation rival with person-luise-kral; condition; flag +argued_with_own_corpse; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Emil学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Emil的材料齐全。年底，Emil从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 25 -> 29; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 47; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1934.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1934.07** `r14-soc.secret-kept::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/forged-the-story` tags: secret_kept, secret, friendship, shame, trust, vienna, urban, crisis
  Marie Huber把一张出生证明摊在桌上，说孩子的父亲不能出现在纸上，求Emil帮忙编一个死在海上的丈夫。Emil在洗衣房的蒸汽里听完整件事，最后把自己的旧姓借了出去。纸上的墨迹干了，Marie Huber的手还在抖。孩子满月那天，Marie Huber送来一篮鸡蛋，篮底压着那张证明的副本。
  _effects: condition; flag +kept_confider_secret; relation friend with person-marie-huber; knowledge “the confider invented a dead husband on a birth certificate”_

  主顾在契约上按了印，要求一把带黄铜镶边的胡桃木书柜。Emil带着Katharina Kraus做了四十天，刨花堆到窗台；交货那天，主顾把尾款数了两遍，又在工匠名册上添了Emil的名字。
  _effects: organization_found 师傅作坊 (household_firm); position 师傅 @workshop:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005422; state_add wc17-master-craftsperson state:world-vienna-1912:281; cash 4 (masterwork commission fee); skill; reputation 0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; flag +masterwork_accepted; knowledge “主顾把尾款数了两遍，并把我的名字写进了工匠名册。”; role; flag +master_craftsperson_

- **1934.08** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Emil刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Emil站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 52; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 1 -> 2; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 3 -> 4; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1934.10** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.12_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005272_

- **1935.01** `r14-edu.sibling-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-failed` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, exam, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Mitzi Kral差三分。回家路上，Mitzi Kral一句话也没说，进了门就把自己关在屋里。Emil把饭菜放在门口，敲了两下，没有回应。半夜，Emil听见屋里有人在翻书，翻到天亮。第二天清早，Mitzi Kral出来洗脸，眼睛肿着，问Emil能不能再教一年。补考费一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (弟弟妹妹的准考证（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +sibling_failed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000427:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003450_

- **1935.01** `bc18-ghost.quiet-correction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/school-inspector` tags: bureaucracy, paperwork, identity, absurd, legal, quiet, comic, vienna
  学务督察按着新户籍找上门，说有一名适龄儿童从未入学。Emil躲在阁楼里，Luise Kral 2在楼下说孩子出疹子，不能见风；督察留下了一张入学通知和一瓶鱼肝油。晚上，Emil从阁楼下来，看见通知上写着“七岁”，忽然觉得自己这一生确实该重新开始。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 47 -> 62; reputation -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; condition; flag +school_notice_for_newborn; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Emil被叫到学校，老师说Emil Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Emil没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 15 -> 10; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 55; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.01** `state_expire:oj18-odd-apprenticeship` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state

- **1935.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Emil的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Emil收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Emil先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 29 -> 36; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 62 -> 72; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:wc17-master-craftsperson/workshop-year/commissions-arrive` tags: wc17-master-craftsperson, craft, work, master, workshop, ownership, reputation, state
  Emil的作坊又接了两单：一扇橡木门、一只修到一半的八音盒。Emil把工钱先付给学徒，自己留下最后一张钞票，用铅笔在账本背面记下欠料行的数目。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.backlog 3 -> 5; cash 2 (workshop commissions); reputation 0.01999999999999999_

- **1935.01** `major-task.marriage-proposal/proposal-refused` tags: task, task:family, family, marriage, courtship, household
  Marie Huber把戒指推回来，说自己的工钱还要寄给乡下的母亲，不能现在就成家。两人在长椅上坐到天黑，最后一起走到电车站；第二天在楼道里碰见，彼此都客气了许多。Emil把戒指重新包进手帕，放回抽屉。
  _effects: flag +marriage_proposal_refused; cash -2 (tram fare and coffee at the riverside); knowledge “对方的工钱要寄回乡下，短期内不能成家。”_

- **1935.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  铅笔头短得捏不住，Emil把它夹在两指之间，在表格背面写下两个名字。被漏掉的人一个叫扬，一个叫弗兰茨，都是每天清早从火车站走来的临时工。工头看了一眼背面，没说话，把表贴回墙上。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1935.03** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Emil把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Anna Bauer放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Mitzi Stein在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1935.10** `r3-war.defeat-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::defeat-rumor-crisis/saw-officer-fleeing` tags: war, rumor, defeat, bread, fear, vienna, urban, crisis
  谣言越传越具体，说皇帝已经跑了。Franz Moser在市政厅门口贴出辟谣告示，浆糊还没干，就被人撕下一角。Emil站在人群里，看见Elisabeth Berg骑着马从街上过，马鞍上绑着一只皮箱；没人再说话。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999976; flag +saw_officer_fleeing_with_suitcase; knowledge “an officer was seen leaving with a packed suitcase”_

- **1936.01** `wc17-sweatshop.the-big-order::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/subcontract-the-beds` tags: sweatshop, ownership, work, factory, order, apprentice, profit, craft
  后街的中间人提出把床架转包出去：Emil只负责验收和交货，刨花和工钱都留给另一间没有招牌的作坊。Emil去看了一眼那间屋子，窗边摆着三张床，孩子们在刨花里数钉子；回来时，Emil把契约签了，却把交货日期往后挪了一周。
  _effects: cash 3 (margin from subcontracting the bed frames); state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.backlog 5 -> 6; reputation -0.01999999999999999; condition; flag +subcontracted_beds; knowledge “后街的作坊没有招牌，窗边摆着三张床，孩子们在刨花里数钉子。”_

- **1936.01** `state_reaction:wc17-master-craftsperson/stolen-pattern` tags: wc17-master-craftsperson, craft, work, master, workshop, ownership, reputation, state
  有人拿走了Emil画在牛皮纸上的榫卯图样，几周后，同样的柜子出现在另一条街的橱窗里，标价更低。Emil没有去告，只在自家门框上重描了一遍印记。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.backlog 6 -> 4; reputation -0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006364_

- **1936.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.no-vacancy` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  工头说眼下没有空缺，让他把名字和住址写在账本撕下的纸角上，说月底再来问。Emil看那张纸被随手压进抽屉，纸角还露出半截，知道多半不会有人翻。他道过谢，往街尾另一家走去。
  _effects: flag +seeking_work_

- **1936.04** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.default` tags: home, market, food, social
  欠账拖了半个月。玛丽亚在菜摊前看见Emil路过，直接喊住要钱。Emil拿不出，玛丽亚把已经称好的土豆倒回筐里。Otto Huber提着篮子站在两步外，什么也没说。
  _effects: reputation -0.15; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1936.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  下午的活是搬纱锭，Emil蹲下去再站起来眼前发黑，扶墙站了一会儿。工友老约瑟夫把自己的水杯推过来，说去诊所开个假条吧，她摇头，说假条要扣钱。
  _effects: condition_

- **1936.09** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000740:child; reputation -0.03_

- **1937.01** `oj18-radio.broadcast::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/stayed-legal` tags: radio, broadcast, antenna, police, night, work, suspense, vienna
  Emil没有上屋顶，只把Katharina Huber的天线图折好，塞进《无线电年鉴》里。第二天，Emil去给税务局修了一台官方收音机，顺手把音量调到最大；走廊里的人都听见了整点报时，没有人听见屋顶上的事。
  _effects: cash 2 (official radio repair fee); reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; flag +stayed_legal; knowledge “税务局那台收音机的声音开得很大，走廊里的人都听见了整点报时。”_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:wc17-master-craftsperson/workshop-year/winter-thin` tags: wc17-master-craftsperson, craft, work, master, workshop, ownership, reputation, state
  冬天来得早，煤价涨了，主顾却要压价。Emil守着冷掉的胶锅，把最后一块好木料留给一单可能退掉的活；账本上的欠款又添了一行。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.debt 2 -> 5; cash -1 (workshop winter costs（其中1克朗记入债务）)_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `r13-work.find-supplier::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cash-only` tags: commerce, supply, credit, shop, negotiation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Berta Huber 2把样布收回去，说新铺子一律现钱。Emil把口袋里的钱数了两遍，只够买半匹，剩下的钱还要留着交电费。Berta Huber 2见Emil数得仔细，让了半步，说可以先拿一匹，下次必须现结。
  _effects: cash -8 (后巷仓库的样布（其中8克朗记入债务）); flag +work13_bought_supplies_cash_only; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

- **1937.07** `fa17-cabaret.debut::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/booed-off` tags: art, performance, cabaret, debut, reputation, failure, theatre, vienna
  Emil的假发在第二段副歌时滑到耳边。前排有人喊“下去吧”，随后整个大厅都跟着拍手，节奏比乐队还齐。Rosa Moser在幕布后没有出来，Emil自己走回后台，经过化妆镜时看见脸上还留着半层没卸干净的粉。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:350; state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:351; reputation -0.06; flag +booed_on_debut; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1937.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil把通知念给Anton听，Anton摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Emil去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1937.12** `r13-turn.recovery::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/healed-with-a-limp` tags: health, recovery, medicine, body, hope, vienna, urban, crisis
  伤口长好了，腿却短了一截。Josef Winter说骨头接歪了，要重新打断再接，Emil问要多少钱，Josef Winter没有答。Emil拄着拐杖走出医院，在台阶上坐了一会儿，看别人两条腿下楼梯，自己数着台阶一级一级挪。
  _effects: cash -4 (拆掉绷带的那天（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.04 (拆掉绷带的那天); condition; flag +healed_with_permanent_limp; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Emil在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Josef记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Emil带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1938.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-born-at-home` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Marie Huber在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个孩子，取名Grete Bauer。Emil在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Emil从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee); role; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:369_

- **1938.01** `pl19-bridge.public-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-back-room-opens` tags: scandal, protection, network, press, media, rumor, bridge, vienna
  报纸卖完的第二天，Otto Huber把Emil领进洗衣房后面的小间。桌上已经坐着门房、一位排字工和两个不肯说名字的人；他们没有问绯闻是真是假，只问Emil以后还打不打算出门。Emil说打算，Otto Huber就把一盏灯往桌中间推了推。
  _effects: state_add pl19-protected-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:370; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; reputation -0.02; flag +rumor_network; knowledge “洗衣房后间的人没有问绯闻真假，只问当事人还出不出门。”; flag +protected_scandal_active_

- **1938.01** `state_reaction:public-scandal/gossip` tags: public-scandal, scandal, reputation, social, state, state_reaction, protection, network
  Emil走进咖啡馆时，邻桌的声音低了一拍。没有人当面说什么，但每个人都知道该说什么。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.03_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007284_

- **1938.01** `state_reaction:pl19-protected-scandal/a-public-question` tags: pl19-protected-scandal, love, secret, scandal, power, protection, network, protected_scandal
  有人在一场公开活动里问起Emil的“私人安排”，语气客气，问题却像一根针。Emil笑着答了别的事，回家后才发现手心全是汗。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.publicHeat 45 -> 57; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 55 -> 49; reputation -0.03_

  画店老板把Emil的作品推回来，说尺寸不合墙面，题材也不合买主的口味。Emil抱着画走回阁楼，路上经过一家亮着灯的咖啡馆，玻璃里映出一个抱着旧画框的人，像一个迟到的搬运工。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 70 -> 77; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 5 -> 4; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.integrity 45 -> 42_

- **1938.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.pay-deposit` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Emil从内袋里数出两克朗压在柜台上，Berta Huber 2翻开借书卡，用铅笔把上次迟还的四天圈出来，还是把小说递了过来。Emil把书塞进外套里侧，出门就翻到第一页，边走边读，差点撞上推手推车的菜贩；两克朗换来的不只是一本小说，还有一整个下午的安静。
  _effects: cash -2 (租书店借小说的押金); skill; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1938.03** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/term-ends-clean` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  任期在十二月三十一日结束。Emil把印章擦干净，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把；Elisabeth Berg来接手续，两个人把最后一份移交清单对了两遍。走出大门时，门房照旧叫了一声“先生”，没有加头衔。
  _effects: position_end 无线电修理匠 @radio:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:004510; reputation 0.020000000000000004; condition; flag +left_office_in_order; knowledge “移交清单上最后一项是印章，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把。”_

  房产经纪人只用了两天就找到买主，要把烟铺改成一家卖进口香皂的店面。Emil在契约上签了字，拿到十二克朗，比继承来的赊账多出不少。Josef Bauer和她的姐姐在月底搬去城郊的侄女家，临走前把猫留在空柜台下面；Emil没有回去看那只猫，只在账本最后一页写下“已结清”。
  _effects: cash 12 (卖掉烟铺的钱); relation_end kin with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; reputation -0.05; condition; flag +sold_the_tobacco_shop; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:wc17-master-craftsperson/workshop-year/winter-thin` tags: wc17-master-craftsperson, craft, work, master, workshop, ownership, reputation, state
  冬天来得早，煤价涨了，主顾却要压价。Emil守着冷掉的胶锅，把最后一块好木料留给一单可能退掉的活；账本上的欠款又添了一行。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.debt 5 -> 8; cash -1 (workshop winter costs); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `state_expire:public-scandal` tags: public-scandal, scandal, reputation, social, state, state_tick, state:public-scandal
  Emil的「公开丑闻」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:351_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:pl19-protected-scandal/the-network-meets/someone-wants-a-favor` tags: pl19-protected-scandal, love, secret, scandal, power, protection, network, protected_scandal
  有人替Emil挡了一次采访，随后在走廊里提起自己儿子的学徒期满。Emil知道这不是闲聊；当天晚上，那张写着名字的纸条被压在糖罐下面，像一枚没有付清的硬币。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.leverage 15 -> 27; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 49 -> 45; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 55 -> 60; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/kin-nursed` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, crisis
  Marie Huber把粥熬得很稀，一勺一勺吹凉，托着Emil的后颈喂下去。窗帘拉开半尺，太阳从床脚移到墙上，Emil第一次觉得退烧后的骨头是轻的。Marie Huber把药钱记在日历背面，说下个月少买两次肉。
  _effects: cash -2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); food; health 0.07999999999999999 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_kin_care; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1939.03** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/jam-at-door` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil只送来一罐杏子果酱，没有进门，把罐子放在门槛里边，说了句汤你们先喝。Anna Bauer在屋里听见了，走到门口时人已经下楼。果酱罐上没有标签，只裹着一层报纸，瓶口用蜡封着；这一罐甜食抵得上一克朗的吃食，却没能把两个人重新按到一张桌上。
  _effects: food; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.hand-to-family` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  家里那间屋子朝北，下午的光只够照亮桌角。Emil把车钱塞进外套内袋，听见母亲在数硬币时嘴里念念有词。师兄说过别到处讲，Emil就没讲，连妹妹问起也只说“够用”。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1940.01** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired-competent` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  Emil把Grete Weiss 2的名字写在编制表的空行上，又在旁边注了“试用三个月”。Anton Stein 2提醒说预算只够半个位置，Emil还是把名字留下了。第二天，Grete Weiss 2提前半小时到，把走廊里的旧档案按年份重新排了一遍。
  _effects: position 助理文书 @workshop:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005422; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; reputation 0.03; flag +hired_protege; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Emil被叫到学校，老师说Franz Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Emil没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 26; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 47; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils)_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Emil把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Emil把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 30 -> 34; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 4 -> 7; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 77 -> 75; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  照片没有登报，却出现在一封没有署名的信里。Emil认出拍照的角度：只有站在后楼梯第二级的人才拍得到。第二天，那个位置换了一把新锁，钥匙却少了一把。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 60 -> 78; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.publicHeat 57 -> 67; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 45 -> 35; reputation -0.04000000000000001; knowledge “后楼梯第二级是唯一能拍到那张照片的位置，而钥匙少了一把。”_

  Emil被叫到学校，老师说Rosa Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Emil没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 18; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `task-vienna-bourgeois-youth.concert-outing/balcony-bread` tags: bourgeois, education, culture, salon, university, family, task, task:culture
  Emil买了两张最便宜的楼座票，一共四克朗。Josef家里起初不准，说星期天要回家吃饭，后来松了口。幕间两人分了一块带来的面包，音乐厅的暖气把外套烘得发烫。
  _effects: cash -4 (两张楼座票); mood 0.08999999999999997; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; skill_

- **1940.02** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Friedrich Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Emil把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/mother-opened-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  回信没有到Emil手里。Rosa Kral的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Emil Winter隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Rosa Kral再见到Emil时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Emil站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1940.12** `major-family.separation-widowhood/separation-widowhood.widowed-tin-box` tags: family, separation, widowhood, housing, major, lifecycle, death, injury
  Marie Huber咳了半年，最后一个月连楼梯都上不去。Emil把床让给对方，自己睡在厨房的长凳上。Marie Huber死在清晨，Emil去市政办事处登记，又去Marie Huber上工的地方结清工钱；葬礼从简，神父念完经，Emil把Marie Huber的工票和剃刀收进一只铁盒。
  _effects: death (pulmonary tuberculosis); health -0.06 (nursing and grief before the funeral); role; mood -0.08000000000000007; cash 8 (final wages collected from the partner's workplace); cash -12 (funeral, mass and black clothes)_

- **1940.12** `oj18-mortuary.first-body::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/municipal-mortuary` tags: death, mortuary, funeral, work, odd_job, night, everyday, vienna
  市立太平间缺一个夜班杂役，Luise Moser只问Emil怕不怕冷。地下室的抽屉一排十二个，有的拉开了就不再推回去；Emil的工作是登记遗物、给无名尸挂上编号，再在天亮前把地拖干净。
  _effects: organization_found 市立太平间 (state); position 太平间杂役 @morgue:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:008277; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:439; cash 2 (municipal night wages); condition; flag +mortuary_worker; knowledge “太平间地下室的抽屉一排十二个，无名尸也要有编号。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1941.01** `r14-edu.sibling-favour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sibling-paid-the-fees` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, reunion, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Mitzi Kral在城里站稳了脚，托人带来一个信封，里面是八克朗和一张夜校报名单。信上只写：当年你教我算术，现在轮到我付学费。Emil把报名单看了三遍，填好，第二天一早就去交了。
  _effects: cash 8 (多年后的一封家信); flag +sibling_repaid_tutoring; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000427:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005676_

- **1941.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Emil在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Emil把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008275_

- **1941.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Mitzi的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

  Elisabeth Berg在签字前把一张剧院包厢的票根留在桌上，说是“给孩子的”。Emil把票根推回去，却在许可证上签了名；排水图仍缺一页，但窗口当天就盖了章。扩建开工后，第一场雨把后院淹了半尺。
  _effects: organization_funds; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; flag +permit_for_favor; knowledge “许可证少了一页排水图，盖章时没有补。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  又一年过去，Emil把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Emil把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 34 -> 38; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 7 -> 10; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 75 -> 73; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Emil把Rosa Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Rosa Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Emil听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。

- **1942.01** `r13-fam.write-missing-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/letter-sent` tags: task, family, letter, missing, war, literacy, vienna, urban
  Emil替Luise Moser写信，把最后知道的部队番号、收容站和一处旧住址都写上去。Luise Moser不识字，却要Emil把“家里都好”念三遍，怕写错。信投进邮筒时，她伸手在筒口摸了一下；Emil把回信地址又描粗了一笔。
  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); flag +wrote_to_missing_relative; skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; knowledge “A letter with the last known unit and shelter addresses was ”_

- **1942.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Emil听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Alois Winter 2扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.06000000000000005 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.06** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Gustav Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Emil把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 26 -> 29; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009491_

- **1942.09** `r1-fam.family-reconciliation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/reconciliation.pays` tags: family, reconciliation, hospital, siblings, money, vienna, urban, crisis
  Katharina Bauer住院的第三天，Mitzi Kral来了，手里提着一网兜橙子。两人在走廊里坐了一夜，谁也没有提分家的事。天亮时，Mitzi Kral说医药费他对半；Emil说不用，你把这些年欠母亲的探望补上就行。Mitzi Kral没有回答，把橙子一个一个摆在床头柜上。
  _effects: cash -3 (医院走廊里的两兄弟); flag +hospital_reconciliation; role; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000427:child; knowledge “兄弟第三天来了，提着一网兜橙子。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1942.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Josef Hofer点了牛奶咖啡，Emil要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Emil和Alois Kral在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Alois Kral在门后放了一根木棍，Emil把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

- **1943.01** `wc17-sweatshop.the-big-order::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-order-is-taken` tags: sweatshop, ownership, work, factory, order, apprentice, profit, craft
  旅馆老板Emil Weiss 3把一张订单摊在刨床上：四十张床架，月底交货。Emil算了一遍工时，按现在的做法只能保本；第二天，Emil把Alois Winter 2的午饭时间缩短，又把工钱改成按件计。Alois Winter 2没有反对，只在木料上多画了一道线。
  _effects: state_transition wc17-master-craftsperson -> wc17-sweatshop-owner; cash 4 (hotel order deposit); relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +took_the_big_order; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “四十张床架的利润来自缩短午饭时间和按件压价。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97; role; flag -master_craftsperson; role; flag +sweatshop_owner_

- **1943.01** `state_reaction:wc17-sweatshop-owner/blood-on-the-cloth` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  一个学徒的手指被卷进皮带，血滴在麻布上。Emil让人把她送去诊所，又吩咐把染红的布裁掉；晚上算账时，赔偿金和那批布的钱写在同一页。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 10 -> 20; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 55 -> 53; cash -2 (worker compensation)_

  巡查簿在换班时不见了，第二天却出现在煤筐底下，最后几页被水泡得发皱。管事的说签字不能算数，扣了Emil半个月的夜班钱；Emil没有争，只在新的簿子第一页把日期写得很大。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 14; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 50 -> 40; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +odd_job_scare_

  天色还灰着，Emil手里攥着几张折皱的证明。Rosa Moser让他念车皮上的号码，听见念对了，就朝装卸班一挥手，说先按日结钱，一天两克朗四十分。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ morgue:person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000584:child-evt:world-vienna-1912:005938; condition; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1943.03** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/ignore-the-dream` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, vienna
  Emil醒后只当是晚饭太咸，翻个身又睡了。下午，地窖的水管爆开，煤和旧箱子泡在齐膝的水里。Elisabeth Winter帮着舀水，Emil一边舀一边想起梦里的水位，正好到第二个台阶。
  _effects: cash -4 (泡坏的煤和旧箱子); health -0.030000000000000027 (在冷水里舀了一下午); condition; knowledge “我没有理会梦，地窖真的淹了。”; flag +df18_ignored_flood_dream; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010062_

  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  移交清单少了一把储藏室的钥匙。Emil没有提，Elisabeth Berg也没有问；三个月后，清洁工在储藏室发现两箱以Emil名义订阅的行业期刊，收件地址仍是这间办公室。
  _effects: position_end 师傅 @workshop:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005422; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; flag +kept_office_keys; knowledge “储藏室有两箱以我的名义订阅的期刊，收件地址仍是这间办公室。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

  画店老板把Emil的作品推回来，说尺寸不合墙面，题材也不合买主的口味。Emil抱着画走回阁楼，路上经过一家亮着灯的咖啡馆，玻璃里映出一个抱着旧画框的人，像一个迟到的搬运工。

  第二天，Katharina Berg把外套送回来，说领口里缝着一张名字条，怕原主人家还要。Emil这才想起父亲总在衣服里侧写名字。Katharina Berg把外套搭在椅背上，说改一改还能给孩子穿。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +donated_coat_returned; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000331:child_

  Emil先给Mitzi Kral盛了满满一碗，扁豆和面团结结实实压在勺底，那块熏肉切开分了两份，锅里的吃食折合两克朗。药费的事一句没提，只问要不要再添点汤。Mitzi Kral吃到一半才开口，说上次说话太冲；桌边多摆的那把椅子，最后有人坐下了。
  _effects: relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000427:child; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  检查员在楼梯口闻到了煤烟和汗味，数了数窗边的床位。Emil把罚单折进账本，转身催工头把剩下的活赶完；这一批货的利润刚好抵掉罚款。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.inspectionRisk 65 -> 80; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 50 -> 47; cash -1 (inspection fine（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036_

  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/semmel-no-words` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  在柜台前买了两只 Semmel，递过一只，自己咬另一只，旧账一个字没提。对方接过面包，两人并肩站在店门口，把那点热气吃完。钱的事还横在中间，但对方说星期六照旧来喝咖啡，别又躲着。
  _effects: cash -0.14 (两只 Semmel（其中0.14克朗记入债务）); food; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000440:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1945.09** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home, injury
  Emil托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Katharina Bauer擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Emil晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Katharina Bauer的腿伤有人料理，Emil却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (看护妇料理后伤势好转); health -0.020000000000000004 (夜间加班的疲劳)_

- **1945.10** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-borrow-from-neighbor` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  看门人正把垃圾桶往院角推，见你回来，眼神在你和门牌之间转了一圈。房东收下四克朗，在账本上划掉一行，没提换锁的事。你上楼时腿有点发软，楼梯扶手凉得扎手。
  _effects: cash 4 (向邻居借来补交欠租); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1945.11** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/hungry-winter` tags: war, homefront, shortage, injury
  Emil用配给卡换到半袋面粉和几根冻土豆。锅里煮的是稀汤，邻居送来一小块人造黄油，说孩子们先吃。
  _effects: food; health -0.03999999999999999 (reduced rations); condition_

  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1948.04** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil跑了一趟医院，把Katharina Bauer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Emil回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); flag +parent_hospitalized; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  Emil在面包店排队时把听来的话又讲了一遍，添了“夜里常有男人上楼”一句。到傍晚，整栋楼都在说二楼女房客的事，连看门人都在门口站着看。二楼的门一直关着，Emil回家时才觉得那句话说得太响。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

  _effects: cash 15 (舅父借给的路费); relation kin with person-franz-lechner; flag +owes_uncle_15_kronen_

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002820:child; food_

  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

  Emil整夜没合眼，按时把最后两包药粉喂给Katharina Bauer，水盆换到第五次。天快亮时咳嗽声稀了，老人的呼吸慢慢平下来。Emil在椅子上打了个盹，醒来时炉子灭了，赶到工坊迟了一刻钟，被扣了半天工钱。
  _effects: relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; cash -1.2 (迟到被扣的半天工钱)_

  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-141_

- **1950.08** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Ferdinand Hofer 2手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Ferdinand Hofer 2把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Emil记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1950.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Alois Berg 2嫌Emil的床单滴水滴到Alois Berg 2家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Emil下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Emil把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Alois Berg 2那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

  Emil和Hermann Bauer在第一个酒棚就坐下了，要了一壶新酒，Luise Huber摸出一副磨损的纸牌。三人打到下午，太阳偏西也没再往上爬；Emil输了几个铜板，连酒带牌局一共一克朗五十赫勒，倒赢回一肚子笑话。回程的电车上，Emil靠着窗，酒意上来，差点坐过站。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (酒棚的新酒和牌局输的铜板); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Ferdinand Hofer 2在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Emil把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Emil站起来报出自己车间的计件工价：糊一百个纸盒二十八赫勒，比隔壁车间少六个。屋里一下子静了，Hermann Bauer把草稿推过来，让书记员把这几个数字写在动议第一行。散会时有人拍了拍Emil的肩膀。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  点心纸包搁在膝上，Alois Kral 2说熟人多，慢慢问，别急。Emil回家把这些话学给母亲听，母亲只叹了口气。那包糖后来自己家里分着吃了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147; cash -1 (再买一包糖上门); mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147_

  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

- **1953.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Friedrich Stein抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Emil把床单对折，腾出一半，Friedrich Stein用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.12** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Friedrich Stein把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Friedrich Stein在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。

  Emil本可以绕开，却回头把Helene Bauer从人堆里拖了出来。拳头落在背上，眼镜碎在口袋里；Helene Bauer后来提着一袋土豆上门，站在门口半天没说出话。袋子上还沾着泥，两个人在门口站了很久。土豆最后煮成了一锅汤。

### 4 subject events

- **1915.03** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Lena踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josefine Hofer把Lena抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Lena举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.05_

- **1915.06** `statistical_infection`
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1915.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Lena的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1915.12** `statistical_death/pneumonia` tags: death
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang发起高烧，咳嗽越来越深。邻居帮忙去请医生，医生到时只留下药粉和一张账单；几天后呼吸变得很浅。Anton在场。
  _effects: death (肺炎)_

  晒衣绳下挂着湿床单，孩子们绕着跑，鞋底把水甩到墙上。Anton在第三圈超过Emil Berg，手先碰到墙根。两颗玻璃弹珠进了Anton口袋，外套第二颗纽扣却崩进下水道。Emil Berg从二楼窗口骂了一句，扔下一根别针。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Anton的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Anton在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1923.04** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/strap-raised-then-dropped` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  皮带从门后抽出来，Friedrich Hofer举到半空，停住了。Anton闭着眼等，只听见皮带扣碰在桌沿上。Friedrich Hofer把它放下，说晚饭后不许出门，把碎碗片捡干净。Anton蹲在地上一片片捡，窗外传来同伴的笑声，指尖被瓷片划出一道白口。
  _effects: mood -0.04; health -0.010000000000000009 (手指被瓷片划破); relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1923.11** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷口有人跑过来，肩膀撞在Anton的胳膊上。烟画卡脱手，被风卷到路中间。Anton追了两步，鞋底在湿石板上打滑，手掌先着地。Katharina Berg从后面追上去，在拐角抓住那男孩的袖子，布条撕下来一截，人还是跑了。
  _effects: mood -0.08; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000331:child; condition_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/delivery-paid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  床单叠成四方，压得筐绳勒进肩膀。Anton照着地址找到利奥波德城的两户人家，一户在院子深处，一户要上四层楼。按件算下来是一克朗六十赫勒，Anton把铜币一枚枚码在Luise Berg枕边。Grete Stein在门口看见，只说“下回别敲错门”；家里省下的那份晚饭，让Luise Berg多歇了一天。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (替妹妹送床单的计件工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (背着湿床单走了几个街区); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000060:child; food_

- **1925.02** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Anton在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Anton用牙咬断，重新来。Friedrich Hofer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Anton把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1925.12** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sent-to-country` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Anton没有进厂，也没有留在家里，而是搭了去乡下的火车。姨妈家在葡萄园边上，缺人摘果子。Anton把课本塞进箱底，白天干活，晚上在油灯下认几个字。秋天寄回家的信里，Anton说这里的星星比城里多。车票两克朗，是姨妈先垫的。
  _effects: cash -2 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); flag +left_school_for_country; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/worked-for-dues` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Anton翻遍口袋只有几枚赫勒，便跟管事的说愿替大家抄歌本抵会费。他坐在窗边抄了一下午，手指被墨水染黑，换来的借书权让他捧回一本讲齿轮的旧书，书页里夹着上一个人留下的铅笔字。
  _effects: skill; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; flag +workers_youth_work_trade; mood 0.03_

  成绩单上差两分。Grete Stein说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Friedrich Hofer来签字。Friedrich Hofer在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Anton把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15000000000000002; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1926.09** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/caught-by-the-watchman` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Emil Weiss 2的灯从摩天轮那边晃过来时，Hedwig已经跑没影了，Anton的鞋带却挂在木槽边。Emil Weiss 2把Anton带到园门口，记下住址，说星期一让学校来领人；Anton一路上只担心Hedwig会不会把这件事讲给全班。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; mood -0.039999999999999994; flag +yf18_prater_caught; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; cash -2 (a fine paid at the Prater office); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1926.09** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/wade-in-first` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, rural, agriculture
  多瑙河的浴棚按男女分开，木板缝里能看见对面的人影。Anton穿着借来的毛线泳衣，站在第三间门口不敢下水；Katharina Kral从水里冒出来，头发贴在额头上，说水不冷。Anton走进河里时，满脑子都是自己肋骨上那道疤。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:67; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  Anton在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Katharina Kral三步远的地方拐进面包店。Anton买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。

  Luise Berg把算术本摊在厨房桌上，Anton用豆子摆出十以内的加减。Luise Berg算错时，Anton不骂，只把豆子重新排一遍。两个月后，Luise Berg的听写和算术都及格了，Friedrich Hofer在饭桌上多给Anton盛了半碗汤。
  _effects: flag +tutored_sibling_well; skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000060:child_

  德语作文题是《我的家乡》。Anton写了半页多瑙河，剩下半页写一个总在电车上看书的人。Grete Stein没有打分，只在页边写“感情太多，论据太少”，把作文折好放进自己的公文包；放学后，Anton看见Mitzi在门口等，忽然不敢走过去。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 56 -> 62; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 3 -> 6; skill; reputation -0.010000000000000002; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Anton在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Katharina Kral。这一次对方先点了头，Anton准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Anton才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  Anton解释说路上没下雨，是床单叠得太厚。客户不听，Emil Moser也没帮腔，只把湿床单抖开，让Anton自己晾。Anton把布角一寸寸翻过来，跟Emil Moser说以后会分开叠、不让潮气闷在里头。Emil Moser哼了一声，算是记下了。

  Anton把油印纸交到校长室，Maria Moser正好坐在里面喝茶。校长问纸是从哪儿来的；Anton说在课本里捡的。Grete Stein当天下午被叫走，第二天换了一个代课老师；Anton的拉丁文成绩突然好了起来，教室里却没有人再和Anton说话。
  _effects: reputation 0.019999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +yf18_denounced_teacher; mood -0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Anton翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Klara在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Anton翻回来时，Hedwig把一支烟塞进Anton嘴里，手抖得点不着火。

  Anton把十赫勒放在桌上的铁皮盒里，翻到歌本最后一页，用铅笔写下自己的名字。Hedwig在后面戳Anton的背，说乡下的草地能躺着听云。
  _effects: cash -10 (青年会远足车费); relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

  舞卡上第一支曲子被Emil写走了。Anton在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Emil说没关系，把手放回Anton背后；第三支曲子响起时，Hedwig在门口冲Anton比了个手势，Anton假装没看见。

  Anton在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Katharina Kral三步远的地方拐进面包店。Anton买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。

  Anton回到学校，发现同班同学已经毕业，新班级里没人认识Anton。Anton在操场上站了一下午，最后没有进教室，跟着父亲去了工地。晚上Anton把课本放进箱底，没舍得扔。工地的工头看Anton年纪小，只让Anton搬轻的。课本压在最底下。
  _effects: mood -0.07; flag +dropout_confirmed; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

  Grete Huber看了看Anton的手，说太小，拿不稳凿子。Anton回家没有提这件事，第二天去码头找零工。
  _effects: mood -0.08; flag +apprenticeship_refused_

- **1929.12** `r3-teen.first-love::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/reciprocated` tags: love, courtship, school, tram, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef把回信写在电车月票背面：星期三，六点，第二站。Anton提前半小时到，站在报亭旁边，把那张月票看了十几遍。Josef从电车上下来时，围巾被风吹到脸上，两个人都笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.12; flag +first_love_reciprocated; relation partner with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912_

- **1929.12** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1930.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton的「第一次心动」到期；相关安排结束。

  Emil Hofer说可以先不交会费，但要先跟着去一趟贫民巷。Anton提着一篮旧衣服走了一下午，回来时在登记簿上签了名。Emil Hofer把一枚铜制小徽章别在Anton的衣领上。
  _effects: cash -3 (慈善会登记簿上的空行); reputation 0.05; flag +joined_charity_after_donation; role_

- **1930.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Josef Bauer在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Josef Bauer敲开Anton的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1930.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Anton的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1930.04** `statistical_recovery`
  Anton从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1930.10** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Anton去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber_

- **1930.11** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Grete Stein把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Grete Stein在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1931.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Anton把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Anton读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1931.01** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Emil Weiss在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1931.01** `r13-fam.plan-wedding::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/modest-wedding` tags: task, family, marriage, wedding, household, debt, reform, medical
  Anton和Josef在厨房桌上把亲戚名单划到只剩两桌，又去当铺问了一只银表能当多少。婚礼那天，借来的桌子拼在院子里，汤里只有几片肉；Josef把戒指戴反了，众人笑了一阵，Anton却没有纠正。
  _effects: cash -10 (婚宴要摆几桌); flag +married_at_a_modest_wedding; role; relation partner with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the wedding cost two months of wages”; household_

- **1931.03** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton跑了一趟医院，把Friedrich Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Anton回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.020000000000000018 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06; flag +parent_hospitalized_

- **1931.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.closed` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard, injury
  Anton把窗关上，争吵声立刻变成闷响，像隔着一层棉被。可那男人的一句话还是从缝里钻进来，她躺到床上翻来覆去，数着墙上的水渍，到很晚才睡着。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (被楼下争吵搅得没睡好); mood -0.03_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Anton在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Anton的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `major-task.marriage-proposal/proposal-refused` tags: task, task:family, family, marriage, courtship, household
  Elisabeth Lechner把戒指推回来，说自己的工钱还要寄给乡下的母亲，不能现在就成家。两人在长椅上坐到天黑，最后一起走到电车站；第二天在楼道里碰见，彼此都客气了许多。Anton把戒指重新包进手帕，放回抽屉。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; flag +marriage_proposal_refused; cash -2 (tram fare and coffee at the riverside); knowledge “对方的工钱要寄回乡下，短期内不能成家。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

  Elisabeth Huber在办事处说了Anton一整个下午的坏话，从欠债说到那场没人看见的火灾。旁边的人没有替Anton说一句话。几天后，Anton发现自己的名字从互助会的名单上被划掉了，Elisabeth Huber只说“我也要养家”。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1932.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Ferdinand Bauer嫌Anton的床单滴水滴到Ferdinand Bauer家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Anton下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Anton把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Ferdinand Bauer那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  保险只报销住院费，药费要自己垫。Anton把药单一张张摊在桌上，发现最贵的一瓶是出院那天开的；Elisabeth Weiss在门口等着，手里拿着同一张单子，纸角被汗捏软了。
  _effects: cash -16 (账单上的最后一行); condition; flag +insurance_gap_medical_debt; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; knowledge “the most expensive bottle was prescribed on the discharge da”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Anton的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Anton看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.11999999999999984 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_

  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Anton的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

  Anton说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Emil Weiss把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Emil Weiss在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1933.10** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Bauer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

  Anton把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Anton的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。

  _effects: condition; cash 18 (accident insurance payment assessed after the injury); role; move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

  Anton记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Helene Winter说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Helene Winter陪着Anton去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-helene-winter; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1934.12** `kernel-crisis.task-rescue-neighbor::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/rescue-too-late` tags: task, rescue, fire, neighbor, betrayal, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Anton撞开阁楼门，烟先滚出来，Franz Stein 3倒在门后，手指还扣着门槛。Anton和Berta Huber 2拽着Franz Stein 3的腋下往外拖，拖到楼梯口时人已经没气；梁木跟着塌下，只压住一只烧焦的箱子。消防队赶开围观的人，登记了Anton的名字，Franz Stein 3的名字写在另一张纸上。Anton的袖口烧穿，去诊所包扎花了三克朗，那只箱子留在院子里，谁也没再碰。
  _effects: death (smoke inhalation and burns in a building fire); condition; role; cash -3 (burnt clothes and a doctor visit); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

  _effects: cash -7 (统舱票上的铅笔字); mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +bought_resold_ticket; knowledge “the ticket was resold from a passenger who did not sail”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  Anton把旧练习本翻到空白一页，写下母亲、自己和Luise Berg的名字。十四克朗的药费单，Anton和Luise Berg各摊五克朗，余下四克朗从母亲的养老金里扣；Anton把五克朗硬币一枚枚摞在桌沿，Luise Berg盯着那一栏看了半天，也从围裙口袋里摸出钱来。
  _effects: cash -5 (分摊母亲药费中自己的一份（其中5克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000060:child_

- **1944.07** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.delay` tags: home, market, food, social
  三个克朗拖了九天。玛丽亚收下时数都没数，只把Anton的名字从木牌上抹去，粉笔灰落在卷心菜上。Elisabeth Berg拎着篮子从旁边过去，没停脚。
  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1944.10** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Franz Moser在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/displaced` tags: war, liberation, 1945, migration
  炮火把Anton住的那条街烧掉一半。Anton跟着人群走到城外，在临时收容所登记，床位上铺的是发霉的稻草。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -5 (temporary shelter（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/leave-vienna` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership, migration
  两张车票分开买，Anton在月台这头，Alois Kral在那头。火车开动后两人才在车厢连接处站到一起，谁也没有说话，只把行李往同一张座位底下推。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; cash -12 (train tickets and lodging（其中12克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

- **1945.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.accepted` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  午休的哨子刚响，Josef Winter夹着烟走到门口。Anton报上姓名、住址和干过的活，声音有点发紧。工头嗯了一声，说明早六点来，管一顿午饭，干得好再谈工钱。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1945.11** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.rent-note/rent-note.look-for-cheaper-bed` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Anton没去事务所，只在洗衣房打听更便宜的床位。有人提到利奥波德城另一条街上有一间合住的屋子，一个月少一克朗半，只是要跟三个人共用一间。Anton去看了两趟，量了窗子和床的位置，回来把箱子底下的旧报纸铺开算账。
  _effects: flag +looking_for_cheaper_housing; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1946.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/refuse-to-sign` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  把那张字据推回去，说房租和母亲的药费都在桌上摆着，这名字不能签。前两次的借条从证件夹里翻出来放在一起，摊平让对方看清。对方把纸卷起来收进外套，门带得比平常重，此后在街上碰见只当没认出来。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.03_

- **1946.04** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Anton把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Katharina Bauer替Anton扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.05** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.flour-loan/flour-loan.two-slices-of-bread` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Anton说自家也快见底了，不能再借面粉，临关门前往Luise Moser手里塞了两块面包，说先给孩子垫一垫。Luise Moser攥着面包站了一会儿，说了声谢谢就走了，楼梯上传来脚步和纸袋的磨蹭声。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.06** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/give-place-to-mother` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Alois Winter怀里的孩子脸烧得通红，Anton往后退了半步，把用粉笔写的号码塞进Alois Winter手里。药房伙计收了六十赫勒，给一瓶褐色药水；回家路上Anton一阵阵发冷。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (街角药房买退烧药)_

- **1947.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.deposit-refused` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  “十克朗押金，先交后干。”Katharina Kraus说得很平。Anton把两只口袋翻给人家看，柜台上只落下一张皱车票。工头收回话头，他也不再问，提着帽子走出栅栏。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +seeking_work; reputation -0.009999999999999995; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

- **1947.05** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/serve-first-talk-later` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton先给Luise Berg盛了满满一碗，扁豆和面团结结实实压在勺底，那块熏肉切开分了两份，锅里的吃食折合两克朗。药费的事一句没提，只问要不要再添点汤。Luise Berg吃到一半才开口，说上次说话太冲；桌边多摆的那把椅子，最后有人坐下了。
  _effects: relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000060:child; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1948.01** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/keep-watch-borrow-elsewhere` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你们站在当铺招牌下面，风从巷口灌进来。邻居说他婆娘这个月也要买煤，但两克朗还能匀出来。你把表给他看了一眼，说这表不能当，是爹留下的。他点点头，没再问。
  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱)_

- **1948.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.neighbor` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  邻居老太太的屋里有一股樟脑味，她把孩子放在膝上颠了颠，说先试一星期。Anton每周付一克朗，早晨把孩子裹好送过去，晚上接回来时孩子手里攥着老太太给的干面包皮。
  _effects: cash -1 (每周付邻居老太太的照看费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1948.06** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/stay-home-care` tags: health, family, home
  Anton跟工头请了三天假，在灶上熬粥，给Josefine Hofer换药。工钱少了一截，母亲的腿却慢慢消了肿；夜里Anton把椅子拼在床边，听见母亲呼吸平稳才合眼。
  _effects: cash -4 (请假三天损失的工钱（其中1.85克朗记入债务）); health 0.1 (在家照护后腿肿消退); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1948.08** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.cotton-in-ears` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Anton忍到天亮，第二天去药房买了五分钱的棉花，撕成两小团塞在耳里。夜里的进行曲还剩一点闷响，翻个身也就过去了。此后在楼梯间遇见Emil只是点头，再没有进过那家的门。
  _effects: cash -0.05 (在药房买棉花塞耳朵); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1948.12** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.wash-day/wash-day.wring-blanket` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, laundry, neighbor, household
  两个人拧毯子的时候，Franz Lechner在旁边递肥皂水，说你们俩力气倒合得上。毯子抬上绳子时还在滴水，Anton甩了甩手，看见自己掌纹里嵌着一道一道的布纹。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003283:child_

- **1949.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/settle-at-the-tavern` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  没有签字，陪对方去酒馆把账当面谈清。掌柜的把欠条一张张摆在桌上，一共二十八克朗。有人替着说了几句好话，讲明利息怎么算、按月还多少，请掌柜的宽限到月底。谈下来先还五克朗，余下的立了新据，担保人一栏空着，掌柜的临走时瞪了一眼。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (酒馆里替对方先垫的第一笔款子和两杯酒（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002820:child; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.03** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Anton一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Elisabeth Huber送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1949.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton看Ferdinand Hofer 2盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Ferdinand Hofer 2愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Anton门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Anton打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1950.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/chronic-cough` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton在诊所走廊的第三张长凳上坐到中午，膝上压着挂号纸和一块湿手帕。Otto Huber把听诊器贴上来不到半分钟，说是潮气和烟，开了褐色止咳糖浆，又让下周再来。药费从当天的菜钱里扣走，回家时篮子里只剩半棵卷心菜。
  _effects: cash -3 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +clinic_first_visit; knowledge “the clinic doctor called the cough chronic bronchitis”_

- **1950.02** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.gave-up` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Anton缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.03** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton数出八赫勒交给Rosa Stein 4，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Rosa Stein 4说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Anton盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1950.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton把通知念给Rosa Stein 4听，Rosa Stein 4摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Anton去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); food_

- **1951.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-dies-at-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子出生时已经没有呼吸。Anton去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗，回来时Rosa Stein 4还躺在床上看着天花板；邻居把借来的摇篮搬走了，Anton成了丧子的父亲，屋里没人知道该说什么，只有炉子还在响。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); role_

- **1951.01** `r13-work.unemployment-benefit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-benefit-office/postwar-benefit-granted` tags: work, unemployment, benefit, institution, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  战后新设的失业登记处在一间旧学校里，课桌当柜台用。Luise Moser问Anton战前做什么，又问他有没有被征召。Anton把退伍证明和工厂解雇单一起递过去，Luise Moser在两张纸之间来回看了三遍，最后批了十六周。
  _effects: cash 8 (补助金窗口的第二张表); flag +work13_postwar_benefit_granted; knowledge “the postwar benefit runs longer for returning soldiers”_

- **1951.03** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/mother-returns-to-work` tags: health, family, home
  Josefine Hofer不肯躺着，第三天就去帮人洗衣挣回一克朗。Anton晚上发现她的腿又肿了，那点工钱抵不上药钱；炉边的药布还没干，又得重新煮。
  _effects: cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱)_

- **1951.06** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Anton把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Rosa Stein 4，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Rosa Stein 4点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Anton摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Rosa Stein 4第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Anton只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水)_

- **1952.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Anton和Emil Bauer 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Emil Bauer 2在门后放了一根木棍，Anton把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-160; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-160_

  Anton把十二只木箱码在板车上，用麻绳捆了两道，轮子碾过石子路时，箱角互相磕着响。走到第三道街口，车轴响了一声，Alois Moser蹲下去紧了紧销子。货送到时比约定早半个钟头，店主验完封条，在送货单上画了押。
  _effects: cash 6 (板车上的十二箱); flag +work13_delivered_goods_on_time; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004365:child_

- **1952.03** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/warehouse-sinecure` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, vienna
  Josef Moser 2把Anton的名字推荐给码头仓库，说那里正缺一个“能扛两百斤的人”。Anton去了才知道，岗位是看管一只装满旧账本的暖房，工钱三克朗，主要工作是别让老鼠咬纸。Josef Moser 2听说后，在工会门口堵了三天，问这算哪门子惩罚。
  _effects: cash 3 (仓库暖房的看管工钱); role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129; flag +enemy_recommended_a_sinecure; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129_

- **1952.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Alois Moser借给Anton一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Anton睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); mood -0.04000000000000015_

- **1952.12** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-got-scoop` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  风从教堂的拱廊里灌过来，队伍里没人说话，只听见罐子碰罐子的声音。Rosa Stein 4排在前面，领完没走，站在墙根等Anton，把自己的面包掰了一半递过去。
  _effects: food; health 0.03 (热汤下肚驱了寒气)_

- **1953.01** `p10-migration-tasks.move-district/move-stay-and-bargain` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, moving, district
  Anton最终没有搬，把箱子又拖回楼上，跟旧房东坐在厨房桌边谈了半个钟头。按月付、煤账分两次清，房东要他每周扫一次楼道。孩子不用换学校，母亲的药也还在原来的药房抓，只是房租每月多了八十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -3 (按月先付一部分房租与旧欠煤账（其中3克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

- **1953.03** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-chain-only` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  链子从你指缝里滑下去，落在绒布上几乎没有声音。掌柜说两克朗四十赫勒，你点了点头。出门时你把表掏出来上了几圈弦，它还在走，只是再没法挂在马甲上了。
