  Hedwig在土豆摊前蹲了很久，挑了一磅没有芽眼的。付完钱，手里只剩几个铜板，又去买了盐。围裙兜太浅，Hedwig用下巴压住布边，走两步就低头看一眼。到家Josefine Hofer把土豆倒进盆里，盐包放在灶台边。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Hedwig把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Hedwig踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Hedwig单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill_

- **1924.08** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-one-visit-only` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Hedwig去了一个下午，看那些旧歌本里夹着褪色的圣像卡，工厂来的几个年轻人在最后一排用手指敲长凳打拍子。第二个星期天Hedwig没再去，在院子里帮母亲晾被单。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.009999999999999995; skill_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/delivery-paid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Hedwig背着床单筐走过两条街，肩膀被带子磨出一道红印。第二户人家的女佣数了件数，把工钱用纸包好。Hedwig回家时Luise Berg还躺着，Hedwig把纸包塞到枕头底下，没叫醒人。晚饭时Luise Berg自己坐起来端碗，Hedwig才觉得肩膀没那么沉。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (替妹妹送床单的计件工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (背着湿床单走了几个街区); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000060:child; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1926.01** `agency:care_for_kin` tags: agency, agency:care_for_kin
  Hedwig想照顾Friedrich Moser，但工作脱不开身，只能托邻居带一锅汤回去。
  _effects: mood -0.039999999999999994_

- **1926.04** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/borrow-notes-at-home` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  学费凑不出来，Hedwig就向同屋的学徒借笔记。晚上把灶台边的桌子擦干净，用铅笔头把讲义抄在旧账单背面，Grete Stein允他每两周交一次抄本抵作练习。抄到第三页时灯芯烧短了，字迹开始发颤。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; skill; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1926.06** `yf18-firstlook.atlas-margin::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/write-back-in-pencil` tags: first_love, school, atlas, note, secrecy, rural, agriculture, small_town
  地理课上，Hedwig在地图册的空白处看见一行铅笔字：“多瑙河从维也纳流走，不从这儿。”Hedwig认出是Katharina Kral的笔迹，趁Grete Stein转身，在下面添了一句：“那你往哪儿流？”下课铃响时，Hedwig把地图册塞进Katharina Kral的课桌。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:68; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; flag +yf18_atlas_note; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.08** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/hide-the-bundle` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Hedwig把两件衬衫、一双袜子和小时候的洗礼证明卷进一块旧桌布，塞到床底下。Josefine Hofer在厨房里数硬币，数了三遍都没有抬头；Hedwig躺在黑暗里，听见夜车从远处经过，铁轨的声音一直传到枕头下面。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:69; flag +yf18_bundle_hidden; mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1927.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-second-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  舞卡上第一支曲子被Klara写走了。Hedwig在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Klara说没关系，把手放回Hedwig背后；第三支曲子响起时，Anton在门口冲Hedwig比了个手势，Hedwig假装没看见。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 55 -> 63; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 30 -> 40; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.04_

- **1927.01** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Hedwig把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Hedwig没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 5 -> 13; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 55 -> 53_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002618_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Hedwig在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Katharina Kral。这一次对方先点了头，Hedwig准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Hedwig才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 68 -> 73; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 35 -> 43; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; mood 0.03_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-runaway-plan/three-months-of-preparation/someone-counts-the-savings` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Hedwig藏在糖罐后面的硬币少了一小叠。父亲说家里这个月煤钱不够，先拿去用了；Hedwig看着空出来的那圈灰，什么也没说。那天晚上，Hedwig第一次认真算了一笔账：从维也纳到格拉茨，到底要攒几个冬天。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 13 -> 28; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 53 -> 47; mood -0.03_

- **1927.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-improved` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Luise Berg把算术本摊在厨房桌上，Hedwig用豆子摆出十以内的加减。Luise Berg算错时，Hedwig不骂，只把豆子重新排一遍。两个月后，Luise Berg的听写和算术都及格了，Friedrich Hofer在饭桌上多给Hedwig盛了半碗汤。
  _effects: flag +tutored_sibling_well; skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000060:child_

- **1927.04** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig在衣帽间堵住Mitzi，问那两张票怎么到了Anton手里。Mitzi说Hedwig从来没敢开口，自己只是替Hedwig把话说了；Hedwig把票根揉成一团，塞进Mitzi的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 63 -> 68; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 10; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 40 -> 35; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.02; mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1927.10** `r1-fam.sibling-rivalry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rivalry.neither` tags: family, siblings, rivalry, apprenticeship, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Stein看了看兄弟俩的手，说两个人的指节都不够硬，谁也不收。Friedrich Hofer求了半天，Franz Stein答应让两人一起干杂活，管饭不给钱。Luise Berg干了三天就走了，Hedwig留下来扫地、磨刀，磨到第六个月，Franz Stein才让她碰第一块好木料。
  _effects: cash 1 (同一把刨子); flag +worked_up_from_dogsbody; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000060:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1927.11** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Helene Kral先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Hedwig每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:89; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; education apprenticeship; role_

  Hedwig趁Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne去喝咖啡，跑到街角的工厂检查所，把卸下来的护板指给Maria Moser看。Maria Moser第二天带着卷尺来了，在压印机旁边量了很久；Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne陪着笑，等门一关，就把Hedwig的工具从案子上扫进了木箱。
  _effects: relation employer with person-otto-winter; reputation 0.03; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 11; flag +yf18_reported_press; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

  Hedwig在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Katharina Kral。这一次对方先点了头，Hedwig准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Hedwig才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

- **1928.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Hedwig又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 11 -> 31; skill; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  客户刚把床单接过去，Luise Kral 2就从楼梯口上来，盯着Hedwig看了两秒，说“你不是这家的”。Luise Kral 2当场把工钱收回，说明天要去找Anton的雇主。Hedwig站在门口，手里还攥着空筐绳，回家一路上没吃东西，Anton问起来，只摇了摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.07000000000000002; relation kin with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1928.02** `yf18-friend.inkwell::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/name-the-friend` tags: exam, cheating, friendship, betrayal, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig说Josef把答案抄在墨水瓶底，还答应分给自己一半。Grete Stein把Josef的座位调到第一排；第二天，Hedwig的课桌里多了一张纸条，上面只有一行字：“你以后别走夜路。”Hedwig把纸条交给Grete Stein，事情从此不再是两个人的事。
  _effects: reputation 0.02; flag +yf18_named_friend; mood -0.01999999999999999_

  考试周里，Hedwig把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Hedwig用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 73 -> 70; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 12_

  Josefine Hofer把Hedwig的名字写进一本硬皮簿子，问一句写一句，最后让他按手印。Friedrich Hofer赶来时，Josefine Hofer说带回去好好管教，这次不立案。走出门口，Friedrich Hofer的手一直攥着Hedwig的袖子。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; flag +police_caution_recorded; relation guardian with person-friedrich-hofer; knowledge “the police opened a caution file in the subject’s name”_

  Hedwig跟着Mitzi Lechner走进印刷厂后面的屋子，交了十赫勒会费，在借书簿上按了手印。他挑了一本讲蒸汽机的册子，封面烫金已经磨掉；管理员说下月十赫勒要按时交，书才能续借。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1929.02** `ld18-letter.schoolbook::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/burned-before-the-answer` tags: love, letter, school, secret, queer, courtship, secret_intimacy, reform
  Hedwig在炉子前把信烧了，纸灰卷起来，像一片黑叶子。Josef第二天问有没有话要说，Hedwig说没有。两个人还是一起走了一段路，在面包店门口分开，谁也没有回头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.04000000000000001; flag +burned_the_schoolbook_letter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Hedwig把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Emil没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Hedwig回头看了一眼，Emil正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1930.01** `oj18-perfume.apprentice::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/nose-returns` tags: perfume, apprentice, smell, shop, craft, work, odd_job, reform
  Hedwig感冒了三天，鼻子像塞了棉花。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把十二只小瓶排在柜台上，让Hedwig闭眼闻；前十一只都答错了，最后一只，Hedwig说出“佛手柑和一点旧木头”。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把那只瓶子推到一边，说“记住这个味道，它比你的名字值钱”。
  _effects: state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:158; skill; reputation 0.03; flag +perfume_apprentice; knowledge “第十二只瓶子是佛手柑和旧木头，师傅说这个味道比名字值钱。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1930.01** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Friedrich Winter在午休时把本子摊在Hedwig的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Hedwig从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Friedrich Winter当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; reputation 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1930.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/back-to-work-too-soon` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，她把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Hedwig又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); health -0.09999999999999998 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon_

- **1930.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Josef Stein在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Josef Stein敲开Hedwig的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1931.01** `r15-work.journeyman-test::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed` tags: work, craft, apprentice, exam, journeyman, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig把最后一块接榫敲进去，严丝合缝。Grete Huber没有夸，只把成品翻过来看了看底，然后在自己名字旁边写下Hedwig的名字。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +passed_journeyman_test; state_transition apprenticeship-contract -> journeyman; role_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002964_

- **1931.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Hedwig把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Hedwig读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Franz Stein 4的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1931.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-accusation-fails` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, reform, medical
  Friedrich Hofer在众人面前说Hedwig用诅咒害人。没有人接话，因为上个月Friedrich Hofer也用同样的话说过另一个人。事情没有闹大，但Hedwig发现，从此每次走进那间屋子，都有人先看Hedwig的手。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-friedrich-hofer; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:187; flag +accusation_echoed; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1931.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Friedrich Hofer病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: health -0.17999999999999994 (the curse answers a real betrayal); condition; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 0 -> 1; mood 0.020000000000000004_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004104_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Hedwig的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Hedwig想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 35 -> 19; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; mood -0.05000000000000002; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1931.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/flowers-placed` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, reform, medical
  Hedwig陪Berta Huber 2去公墓，把一把野菊放在新翻的土上。木牌上的名字被雨冲得发白，Berta Huber 2用手指一笔一画描了一遍，描到最后一笔，指腹上全是泥。Hedwig没有劝，只蹲下来把歪掉的木牌扶正。
  _effects: cash -1 (公墓新翻的土); condition; flag +visited_a_grave_with_neighbour; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

- **1931.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  工友咳的时候用手背挡着嘴，指节发红。Hedwig说“先别填”，声音压得很低。旁边有人听见了，没说什么，只把自己那份互助金登记簿往桌里推了推。
  _effects: mood -0.05; health -0.040000000000000036 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1932.01** `r15-work.first-commission::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/haggled-down` tags: work, craft, journeyman, commerce, reputation, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Lechner挑了三处毛病，把价钱压下去一半。Hedwig没有争，因为下个月的房租已经到期。成品被搬走时，Hedwig看见自己的名字还留在底部。
  _effects: cash 2 (第一笔自己的订单); mood -0.03; flag +underpaid_first_commission_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003898_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/dismissed-after-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, unemployment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Hedwig不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Hedwig拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Hedwig的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); health -0.12 (injury and lost job); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Hedwig把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Alois Berg 2没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Hedwig回头看了一眼，Alois Berg 2正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

- **1932.01** `curse15-aftermath.the-inquiry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-parish-clears-it` tags: witchcraft, investigation, reputation, rumor, legal, aftermath, reform, medical
  教区派人来问了两天，最后在登记册上写：无实据。Katharina Moser不肯签字，笔被Franz Moser收走了。Hedwig以为事情到此为止，直到发现邻居开始把自家孩子叫回屋里，只因为Hedwig在门口多站了一会儿。
  _effects: state_remove suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:187; reputation 0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; flag +parish_found_no_evidence; mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1932.01** `kernel-social.lending::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/lending.coal-money` tags: task, task:social, social, lending, debt, neighbor, money, everyday
  Rosa Lechner 2说煤店的账拖不过去，向Hedwig借五克朗。Hedwig从工钱袋里抽出钱，Rosa Lechner 2在烟盒背面写了借条，说发薪日一定还；两人把烟盒压在厨房的盐罐下面。那天晚上，Hedwig家的炉子只烧了半筐煤。
  _effects: cash -5 (lent five kronen for the neighbour’s coal bill); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; role; flag +loan_outstanding; knowledge “借条写在烟盒背面，压在厨房的盐罐下面。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1932.03** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-favor` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, reform
  牌桌上输掉最后一张钞票后，Franz Moser把一叠钱推到Hedwig面前，说不用写借条，只要Hedwig答应“以后有一天帮一个忙”。Hedwig数了数，正好二十克朗。Franz Moser没有说是什么忙，只把烟按灭在桌角，说时候到了会来找Hedwig。
  _effects: cash 20 (牌桌上借来的二十克朗); state_add df18-borrowed-luck state:world-vienna-1912:228; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; flag +df18_took_card_money; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +df18_luck_borrowed; knowledge “我得到了一点不该白拿的好运，代价还没有说清。”_

- **1933.01** `statistical_death/accident` tags: death
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang附近出了事故。工友和路人把人抬到门口，等医生赶到时已经没有了呼吸。Emil Weiss在场。
  _effects: death (意外事故); role; flag -odd_job_apprentice; flag +df18_luck_settled_

- **1918.01** `birth` tags: birth

- **1924.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Gustav Winter把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Gustav Winter踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Gustav Winter单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.01999999999999999 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1924.07** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/home-remedy` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Gustav Winter烧得翻来覆去，Josef Winter把醋倒进温水，用布蘸着擦手心、脚心，又去灶间煮接骨木汁。勺子送到嘴边，Gustav Winter偏头躲，Josef Winter就等一会儿再喂。鸡叫时炉火压小，床脚用旧大衣堵住。两天后热度退了，嘴唇起了一层干皮。
  _effects: health 0.03 (土法退烧，热度渐退); mood -0.06_

- **1925.01** `iw18-home.ration-tin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/tells-mother` tags: childhood, family, money, hunger, secret, school, reform, medical
  Gustav Winter把铁盒端到桌上，说里面的钱对不上，可能是自己数错了。Josef Winter把硬币倒出来，一枚枚排开，排到最后少了一枚；Josef Winter没有问是谁拿的，只说“明天少买一次煤”。Gustav Winter低头把硬币又数了一遍，这次数得很慢。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 27 -> 29; mood -0.02; flag +counted_the_tin_together_

- **1925.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Gustav Winter拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Friedrich Hofer弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Gustav Winter接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

  靴子被Josef Winter收进柜子，说等冬天再穿。第二天，Gustav Winter看见靴子穿在弟弟脚上。Gustav Winter没有问，只在晚饭后把柜门关得很响。Josef Winter听见了，说大的让小的，天经地义。Gustav Winter把自己的旧鞋收进床底。

- **1926.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Gustav Winter前面的大婶一直往后靠，Gustav Winter就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Gustav Winter的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.01999999999999999 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1926.03** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-scarlet` tags: childhood, street, social, home, injury
  巷子里一家接一家地病倒。Gustav Winter烧到说胡话，Josef Winter把阁楼小屋腾出来，在门把手上系了块红布。医生隔着门看了Gustav Winter的喉咙，留下药粉，收下一克朗二十赫勒。Josef Winter把家里留着买煤的钱数了两遍，刚好够。
  _effects: health -0.2 (猩红热); cash -1.2 (付医生诊费); condition_

- **1926.06** `r2-leisure.piano-lesson::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/played-through-the-metronome` tags: leisure, piano, lesson, discipline, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Gustav Winter没有管节拍器，自己数着拍子把这一小节弹完。Grete Stein的铅笔停在半空，等最后一个音落下才敲桌面，说节奏错了，但音乐对了。Josef Winter在隔壁听见，推门问是不是可以考音乐学院。Grete Winter从门缝里学了一句，走调走得比Gustav Winter还准。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; flag +played_through_broken_metronome; role; skill; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000584:child_

- **1927.01** `r1-child.midterm-transfer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-central-index/fathers-signature` tags: school, moving, papers, transfer, class, reform, medical, schooling
  新规定要父亲在转学证上签字。Josef Winter说父亲在战俘营，Katharina Bauer 2说那就等。Josef Winter在门口坐了一下午，最后自己签了父亲的名字，笔迹抖得不像。回家后她把那张纸压在钟底下，再没提过。
  _effects: cash -1 (转学证上少一个章); flag +mother_forged_father_signature; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1927.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-bought-secondhand` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  Josef Winter把八个赫勒排成三摞，最后还差两枚，向摊主说了句好话，才把石板和一支石笔一起拿走。石板背面有一道旧划痕，Gustav Winter用指甲抠了抠，抠不掉。回家路上Gustav Winter把石板贴在胸口，隔着布也能感觉到石头的凉。
  _effects: cash -0.08 (买二手石板和石笔); skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1927.02** `iw18-secret.cellar-door::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-door-was-just-coal` tags: childhood, secret, family, cellar, knowledge, fear, reform, medical
  地窖里只有煤、旧箱子和一只缺腿的凳子，Gustav Winter转了一圈，什么秘密也没找到。上楼时Josef Winter正在切洋葱，问孩子下去做什么；Gustav Winter说找坛子，Josef Winter便让孩子把手洗了来帮忙，案板上的洋葱辣得两人都流泪。
  _effects: mood 0.010000000000000002; food; flag +cellar_was_only_coal_

- **1927.02** `iw18-play.toy-theatre::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-audience-claps` tags: childhood, play, theatre, family, wonder, comic, reform, medical
  Gustav Winter用鞋盒搭了一个戏台，把画在硬纸上的国王、仆人和一条狗插在缝里。Josef Winter坐在床沿当观众，看到国王被狗追下台时笑出了声。Gustav Winter谢幕三次，最后把那只狗也抱出来，说它才是主角。
  _effects: mood 0.05; skill; reputation 0.009999999999999995; flag +toy_theatre_performed_

- **1927.08** `iw18-play.kite-on-roof::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-lodger-gets-it-down` tags: childhood, play, kite, roof, neighbor, comic, reform, medical
  楼里的一个大人搬来长梯，把风筝从烟囱边挑下来。Gustav Winter在下面张着手，Grete Winter负责扶梯子；风筝落到地上时，尾巴上多了一层黑灰。大人拍拍手走了，Gustav Winter追到楼梯口，才发现自己忘了说谢谢。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000013; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000584:child; flag +adult_rescued_kite_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-school-day/recites` tags: childhood, school, hunger, injury
  Grete Stein让Gustav Winter站到黑板前背乘法表。孩子背到七乘八时停了一下，还是背完了；放学时手指冻得发红，石板上的字被袖子擦掉一半，Grete Stein在名册上给了一个小小的勾，Gustav Winter把石板抱在胸前，一路没舍得放进书包。
  _effects: education primary; health -0.010000000000000009 (cold classroom)_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:lc17-child-prodigy/practice-year/sits-with-hands-folded` tags: lc17-child-prodigy, childhood, promise, talent, pressure, family, education, prodigy
  冬天屋里冷，Gustav Winter把手指压在暖气片上，等关节活动开才肯碰琴键。老师上周留下的曲子还停在第三页；Gustav Winter听见楼下孩子在雪地里喊，便把琴盖合上，坐到窗边去看，直到手指重新变凉。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 30 -> 36; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 77 -> 71; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 49 -> 47; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1928.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  土豆滚进墙角的煤灰里，Gustav Winter弯腰去追，再抬头时Elisabeth Stein已经坐在井台边，裤腿湿了一片，哭声一抽一抽。Helene Winter把孩子抱进灶间，Gustav Winter跟在后面，手指抠着门框。Josef Winter回来没问经过，只把Gustav Winter的碗收走一半，说“看个人都看不住”。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.06; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1928.05** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Gustav Winter每天放学都在柜台前站一会儿，看玻璃板底下压着的烟画，顺手把歪了的报纸摞齐。Mitzi Kral擦完柜台，从抽屉最里面摸出一张揉皱的风景卡，边角还沾着烟丝，塞过来时说了句“别挡着门”。Gustav Winter把卡夹进课本，第二天带了一张重复的来给Mitzi Kral看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.020000000000000004_

- **1928.09** `r1-child.reading-timetable::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-reading-room/recognised-the-middle-character` tags: literacy, tram, street, school, breakthrough, reform, medical, schooling
  Gustav Winter每天在电车站等车，盯着站牌上的三个字看。某天忽然认出中间那个是“街”，因为老师在黑板上写过。Gustav Winter沿着站牌一路认过去，坐过了一站，回家晚了半小时。Josef Winter要打，Gustav Winter说我会念站名了。
  _effects: mood 0.06; flag +read_street_sign; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1928.10** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.win` tags: school, social, childhood
  拼写比赛结束时，Grete Stein敲了敲讲台说“记住这个词”。Gustav Winter袖口上多了一枚小十字章，回家路上Grete Winter追着要看，最后用三颗李子换了眼福。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; mood 0.10000000000000003; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; food_

- **1929.01** `lc17-prodigy.recital::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/clean-hands` tags: childhood, prodigy, performance, practice, family, shame, music, reform
  客厅里坐了十二个人，壁炉上的钟慢了一刻钟。Gustav Winter把曲子从头拉到尾，中间只错了一个音；Grete Stein没有笑，只在客人走后把谱子翻到下一页。厨房里端来剩下的杏仁饼，Gustav Winter拿了一块，发现手心全是汗。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 47 -> 53; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 36 -> 40; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 71 -> 74; mood 0.04999999999999999; reputation 0.03; skill; cash 1 (an envelope from the host); relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1929.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Franz Stein 3朝Gustav Winter招手。Gustav Winter挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Gustav Winter把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1929.03** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Grete Stein从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Gustav Winter答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Gustav Winter回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Grete Stein看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1929.04** `r1-child.rural-winter-stove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/teacher-boards-at-home` tags: rural, school, winter, fuel, teacher, reform, medical, schooling
  雪太大，Grete Stein回不了城，住到Gustav Winter家。晚上Grete Stein在灶边改作业，Gustav Winter看见自己的作文被红笔圈了三处。Josef Winter把唯一的鸡蛋煎了，放在老师碗里，Gustav Winter假装没看见。
  _effects: food; flag +teacher_boarded_at_home; skill; relation friend with person-grete-stein_

- **1929.11** `r14-edu.boarding-arrival::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/cold-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_isolation, boarding_school, childhood, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Maria Moser把Gustav Winter领到三楼的宿舍，指着一张靠窗的铁床，说被子每周三换。床垫上有一道凹痕，是上一个学生留下的。Luise Berg在对面铺被子，没有抬头。窗外是操场，雾很大，旗杆只露出半截。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (宿舍楼下的铁皮箱); mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +boarding_school_arrival; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000060:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

  冬天屋里冷，Gustav Winter把手指压在暖气片上，等关节活动开才肯碰琴键。老师上周留下的曲子还停在第三页；Gustav Winter听见楼下孩子在雪地里喊，便把琴盖合上，坐到窗边去看，直到手指重新变凉。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 40 -> 46; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 74 -> 68; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 53 -> 51; mood -0.04_

- **1930.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/sums-finished` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Gustav Winter把煤油灯往桌子中间挪，让Elisabeth Stein看清算术本上的格子。孩子把七加八数了三遍，Gustav Winter没有直接说答案，只把豆子一颗颗排在桌上。最后一页写完时，灯芯已经烧黑，Gustav Winter才发现自己的作业还空着半页。
  _effects: flag +helped_sibling_with_homework; role; skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001619:child; skill_

- **1930.02** `r3-teen.first-cigarette::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/coughs` tags: smoking, friendship, risk, family, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Berg把烟卷塞进Gustav Winter嘴里，划了根火柴。第一口呛得Gustav Winter眼泪直流，第二口装作没事。Luise Berg笑得蹲在地上，说城里人都这么学。Gustav Winter把烟夹在指间，直到烧到指节才扔掉。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (防火梯上的第一口烟); flag +tried_first_cigarette; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000060:child_

  图纸上画了坡道，预算却只够修一半。Gustav Winter还是每天提前二十分钟到校，让同学把轮椅抬上台阶。Elisabeth Berg说下学期一定修好，Maria Bauer把这句话写进家长通知里。
  _effects: mood -0.04; condition; flag +school_ramp_still_unbuilt; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1930.04** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-leaving-age/straight-to-factory` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  Grete Stein把Gustav Winter的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Gustav Winter。Gustav Winter进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); health -0.06000000000000005 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role_

- **1930.09** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/retake-fee` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  成绩单上差两分。Maria Bauer说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Josef Winter来签字。Josef Winter在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Gustav Winter把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

  Gustav Winter在自习课上写信，写家里那条狗，写宿舍的冷，写Maria Moser查房时手电筒的光。写到一半，Luise Berg探头来看，Gustav Winter把纸捂住。信最后没有寄出去，折成小方块，塞在枕头套里，夜里翻身时能听见纸响。
  _effects: mood -0.05; flag +wrote_unsent_letter; skill_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003340_

  Gustav Winter在自习课上写信，写家里那条狗，写宿舍的冷，写Helene Kral查房时手电筒的光。写到一半，Luise Berg探头来看，Gustav Winter把纸捂住。信最后没有寄出去，折成小方块，塞在枕头套里，夜里翻身时能听见纸响。

  下一站，Gustav Winter追下车，把那只手套递还给Grete Winter。Grete Winter说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Gustav Winter回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:196; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000584:child; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  补考那天，Gustav Winter在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Maria Bauer跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Gustav Winter去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; education apprenticeship_

- **1931.11** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/go-to-brickworks` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social, injury
  “下周一，跟街尾的工头走。”Josef Winter说完就把算术本推到桌角。Gustav Winter知道再争也没用。Maria Bauer在门口拦住Gustav Winter，把一张夜校地址塞过来，说晚上可以去。Gustav Winter把纸条折进鞋垫，第二天就去了砖厂。头一周四克朗，搬砖搬得腰直不起来，纸条在鞋里磨得发毛。
  _effects: role; cash 4 ({subject}砖厂首周工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (砖厂头几天搬砖); flag +has-evening-school-address; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1932.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/give-them-away` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Gustav Winter把两张票送给门口卖花的小女孩，自己在普拉特大街上看橱窗。橱窗里摆着一双和Franz Kral一样的白手套；Gustav Winter站到商店关门，回家时说票弄丢了，谁也没有再问。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 37; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.secret true -> true; flag +yf18_gave_tickets_away; mood -0.020000000000000004_

  Gustav Winter的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Luise Berg骂了一句，Grete Winter却把围巾解下来递给Gustav Winter；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 37 -> 40; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 20; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000584:child; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000060:child; mood -0.009999999999999995_

  Gustav Winter在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Grete Winter。这一次对方先点了头，Gustav Winter准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Gustav Winter才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  Gustav Winter今年自己把练习时间从半小时加到三刻钟。琴谱的边角被翻得起毛，厨房里有人听见同一段反复了七遍，最后一遍终于没有错音；Gustav Winter停下来，才发现窗外天已经黑了，煤气灯在玻璃上照出一小块黄色的自己。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 51 -> 54; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 68 -> 72; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 46 -> 44; skill; mood 0.02_

  Elisabeth Stein把铅笔一摔，说再也不想看见豆子。Gustav Winter把豆子一颗颗收进碗里，说随你。那天晚上，Elisabeth Stein自己把错题改了一遍，改完把本子推到Gustav Winter面前，不说话。Gustav Winter看了一遍，在页角画了一个很小的勾。
  _effects: flag +sibling_resented_tutoring; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001619:child_

- **1932.05** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/buy-the-ticket-tonight` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Gustav Winter趁Josef Winter去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Gustav Winter趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Gustav Winter说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:238; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.039999999999999994; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  Grete Stein把Gustav Winter的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Gustav Winter。Gustav Winter进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); health -0.06000000000000005 (毕业钟响过以后)_

  Gustav Winter在自习课上写信，写家里那条狗，写宿舍的冷，写Friedrich Hofer查房时手电筒的光。写到一半，Luise Berg探头来看，Gustav Winter把纸捂住。信最后没有寄出去，折成小方块，塞在枕头套里，夜里翻身时能听见纸响。
  _effects: mood -0.05; skill_

- **1933.03** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/ride-in-the-dark` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, reform, medical, schooling
  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Gustav Winter和Grete Winter从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Luise Kral提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.05; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman); reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000584:child; flag +yf18_prater_stunt_

- **1933.08** `r3-teen.bullying-revenge::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/report` tags: school, bullying, revenge, violence, class, reform, medical, schooling
  Gustav Winter把Mitzi Kral堵在厕所门口要钱的事写在纸上，交给Grete Stein。Grete Stein看完，只把两人的座位调开，说再观察。Mitzi Kral经过Gustav Winter桌边时，把书包带甩在他的后颈上。
  _effects: flag +reported_bullying; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000427:child; knowledge “the teacher chose to separate seats instead of punishing the”_

- **1933.11** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refuse-the-machine` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, reform, medical
  教堂年历的订单催得紧，Allgemeines Krankenhaus Wien把压印机的护板卸了，说这样进纸快一半。Gustav Winter看着那块铁板，说手会进去；Allgemeines Krankenhaus Wien把工钱袋往桌上一扔，问Gustav Winter是不是不想出师了。Gustav Winter没有碰机器，那天下午的活由Marie Huber接手。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 30 -> 25; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; flag +yf18_refused_unguarded_press; mood 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

  _effects: education higher; state_remove apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:195; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 54 -> 62; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 44 -> 50; cash -2 (exam fees and new strings); mood 0.06; flag +conservatory_accepted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; role_

  考试周里，Gustav Winter把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Gustav Winter用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。

  _effects: reputation -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; flag +yf18_finished_solo; mood 0.009999999999999995_

  Gustav Winter把信放在晚饭桌上。Josef Winter读完，问格拉茨远不远，又说家里的房租下个月要涨；那晚谁也没有说行或不行，信被压在糖罐下面，第二天边角沾了咖啡。

  Gustav Winter趁Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung去喝咖啡，跑到街角的工厂检查所，把卸下来的护板指给Elisabeth Berg看。Elisabeth Berg第二天带着卷尺来了，在压印机旁边量了很久；Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung陪着笑，等门一关，就把Gustav Winter的工具从案子上扫进了木箱。

  Gustav Winter把包袱重新塞回床底，又在上面压了一双冬鞋。母亲进来换床单时，Gustav Winter站在门口，假装在看墙上的圣像；床单抖开的声音像一列很慢的火车，Gustav Winter数着它经过的每一节车厢。

- **1935.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/delivery-paid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Gustav Winter背着床单筐走过两条街，肩膀被带子磨出一道红印。第二户人家的女佣数了件数，把工钱用纸包好。Gustav Winter回家时Elisabeth Stein还躺着，Gustav Winter把纸包塞到枕头底下，没叫醒人。晚饭时Elisabeth Stein自己坐起来端碗，Gustav Winter才觉得肩膀没那么沉。

  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Gustav Winter把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Gustav Winter没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。

  Gustav Winter把油印纸交到校长室，Franz Moser正好坐在里面喝茶。校长问纸是从哪儿来的；Gustav Winter说在课本里捡的。Grete Stein当天下午被叫走，第二天换了一个代课老师；Gustav Winter的拉丁文成绩突然好了起来，教室里却没有人再和Gustav Winter说话。
  _effects: reputation 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +yf18_denounced_teacher; mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  欠了兩個月学费，Maria Bauer把Gustav Winter的座位调到最后一排，说书本先留下。Elisabeth Weiss在门口拦住Gustav Winter，说学校不是救济院。Gustav Winter回家没有说，第二天照常出门，在火车站坐到放学时间。Josef Winter后来在书包里发现了一张空白的退学单。
  _effects: mood -0.14; condition; flag +expelled_from_parish_school_for_arrears; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; knowledge “two months of unpaid fees can cost a child his seat”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

- **1936.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Gustav Winter把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Klara没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Gustav Winter回头看了一眼，Klara正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

  Gustav Winter收到同学会的请柬，看了两遍，放回信封。那天下午，Gustav Winter照常去店里上工，把货架上的罐头摆成一排。傍晚有人问起学校的事，Gustav Winter说记不清了，只记得宿舍楼下的那棵栗树，秋天落一地刺壳。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +avoided_boarding_reunion_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005177_

  Berta Berg把账本塞进钢琴后面的夹层，说等风声过去再拿出来。Luise Moser第二次来时带了一个记录员和一张搜查令；钢琴被挪开时，琴键响了一串不成调的音。Gustav Winter看见黑皮本翻开的那一页上，已经用红笔圈出了三笔利息。
  _effects: cash -10 (the accountant's fee for hiding the ledger); state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:554; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; flag +tax_ledger_hidden; knowledge “黑皮本上被红笔圈出的三笔利息都没有报税。”; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  复核结果下来，申诉驳回，理由是“未提出新事实”。Franz Winter把新的离境日期念了一遍，Katharina Berg站在楼梯口，说愿意作证Gustav Winter在这里住了九年。Elisabeth Berg把证词夹进档案，说“九年不算新事实”。
  _effects: mood -0.15999999999999998; condition; flag +appeal_dismissed; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000331:child; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; knowledge “nine years of residence is not new evidence”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1944.01** `r14-edu.sibling-favour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sibling-fell-ill` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, reunion, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Elisabeth Stein病倒的消息是邻居带来的。Gustav Winter赶到时，屋里只有一张床和一只药罐，孩子坐在门槛上。Gustav Winter留下来照顾了半个月，把自己的夜校报名费垫进了药钱。Elisabeth Stein退烧那天，叫了Gustav Winter一声小名。药钱六克朗，是Gustav Winter垫的。
  _effects: cash -6 (多年后的一封家信（其中6克朗记入债务）); flag +cared_for_sick_sibling; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001619:child_

  Gustav Winter在《新维也纳日报》的失物栏里登了三行小字，花了四十个铜板。第二天，一个卖旧货的人说在电车终点站见过那样的铜牌。Katharina Winter放学后跟着Gustav Winter去找，一路上把书包带绕在手指上，绕得发白。
  _effects: cash -1 (在报纸上登失物启事); state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:586; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006612:child; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +advertised_for_the_childs_medal; flag +something_is_missing_

  Gustav Winter的「被调查」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:554_

- **1944.01** `r13-fam.leave-abusive-partner::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/left-in-the-night` tags: task, health, domestic_violence, safety, partner, shelter, vienna, urban
  Gustav Winter等Rosa Kral睡熟，才把证件和一件换洗衣裳塞进布包。Luise Huber在楼梯口等着，替Gustav Winter提着鞋，两人光脚走过三段楼梯。到了街上，Gustav Winter才发现自己的手一直在抖，布包的带子勒进掌心。
  _effects: cash -3 (门闩从里面插上（其中1克朗记入债务）); health -0.020000000000000004 (门闩从里面插上); condition; flag +left_abusive_partner_at_night; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1944.04** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household
  Gustav Winter把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Gustav Winter的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart（其中6.3克朗记入债务）)_

  Gustav Winter和Rosa Kral开始在咖啡馆后门见面。每次只有半个钟头，说的都是不能在家里说的话；Gustav Winter回家前会在街角站一会儿，把围巾重新系好，确认身上没有别人的烟味。
  _effects: state_add forbidden-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:587; mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +secret_attachment_active_

  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Gustav Winter在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金（其中12克朗记入债务）); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-karl-kraus_

  Gustav Winter在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Gustav Winter把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Gustav Winter在战后第二个月病倒，医院没有足够的药，医生只给了退烧粉。邻居每天送来一碗汤，放在门口就走。
  _effects: health -0.05 (postwar illness); cash -4 (medicine（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Gustav Winter和Friedrich Stein在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Friedrich Stein在门后放了一根木棍，Gustav Winter把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

  Gustav Winter把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Gustav Winter忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。

  Gustav Winter把那段关系继续藏在日常的缝隙里。一次擦肩、一张没有署名的纸条、借来的半个钟头，都足够撑过这个月；回家前Gustav Winter在街角站了一会儿，把围巾重新系好。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 10 -> 5; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.reciprocity 55 -> 58; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Friedrich Stein在开奖前偷偷去了烟草铺，用买菜剩下的钱买了那组号码，把票藏在糖罐后面。号码中了，Friedrich Stein把票放在餐桌上，说本来想等Gustav Winter生日再讲。Gustav Winter先是不信，随后笑得把椅子往后推了一截；两个人坐在厨房里数奖金，数到一半又停下来烧水。
  _effects: cash 70 (the lottery prize bought with the grocery money); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:616; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; reputation 0.02999999999999997; flag +partner_bought_the_ticket; mood 0.09999999999999998; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  过了期限，Anton Weiss把银表摆进拍卖柜，起价是当初借款的一半。Gustav Winter站在人群后面，看见一个陌生人举了三次牌。Emil在门口登记买主姓名，问Gustav Winter是不是来赎当的；Gustav Winter说不是，只是来看看表走得准不准。
  _effects: cash -4 (过期的当票（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.14; condition; flag +pawned_watch_sold_at_auction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; knowledge “the watch sold for half the original loan”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

  诊所设在街角一间改过的铺面里，门口挂着一块红砖色的木牌。Elisabeth Berg每周来两个下午，给孩子量体重、给老人听肺；Anna Bauer送来一盆天竺葵，Emil把候诊室的长凳刨平。Gustav Winter没有剪彩，只把第一张药方折好，放进抽屉最里面。

  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 5 -> 35; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.grief 0 -> 10; reputation -0.07999999999999996; mood -0.04999999999999999_

  Gustav Winter只喝了半杯就看见门口站着等位的老主顾，手里转着帽子。Gustav Winter把报纸叠好放回木架，起身让出窗边的位子，一杯 Melange 三十赫勒，侍者点头致意，没再收第二杯的钱。走出门时雨小了些，Gustav Winter站在檐下理了理衣领，觉得这半个下午也不算白来。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (一杯 Melange 的价钱); reputation 0.030000000000000027; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  遗嘱里还附了一笔丧葬费，条件是要有“家属”到场。死者没有亲人，Gustav Winter只好穿黑衣站在墓旁，听牧师念一个从未听过的名字。雨把花圈上的纸花打湿，泥水漫过鞋面；葬礼结束后，Franz Winter把九克朗交给他，说账已结清。Gustav Winter回家脱鞋，发现袜子上沾着别人的土。

  毯子吸饱了水，沉得像一条死狗。Franz Winter说这毯子是她婆婆的嫁妆，一年只洗一回。Gustav Winter咬着牙拧到最后一圈，水从毯缝里挤出来，在脚边汇成一小片温热。

  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1947.09** `r2-law.eviction-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::r2-law.eviction-hearing-reform/rent_control_defense` tags: law, eviction, housing, court, tenement, vienna, urban, postwar
  新设的租金法庭只看两件事：租金是否超过管制线，房屋是否适合居住。Luise Kral让Franz Stein出示登记卡，Franz Stein拿不出来；Gustav Winter把登记处的回执递上去，租金正好超了三成。Luise Kral判多收部分抵扣欠租，Franz Stein当场说要上诉。
  _effects: cash 4 (驱逐听证的十五分钟); flag +used_rent_control_defense_in_eviction; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; knowledge “unregistered rent above the control line can be offset again”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1948.01** `ml18-family.fortune-split::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/split-it-equally` tags: family, money, windfall, inheritance, conflict, dark, vienna, urban
  星期天的晚饭多摆了一副刀叉。Grete Winter带着一本记账簿来，说钱是大家的运气，不该只落在一个人家里。Gustav Winter把二十四克朗分成三份，Grete Winter数了两遍；Friedrich Stein把烤肉端上桌，谁也没有再提那本账簿。
  _effects: cash -24 (the windfall was divided among the family); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 80 -> 56; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 6 -> 8; flag +fortune_split_equally; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Gustav Winter和那人在剧院后门分开时，被楼上的住户看见了。第二天，邻居的问话里多了一个不该出现的名字；Gustav Winter没有承认，只把外套挂到柜子最里面。

- **1948.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/kin-nursed` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Friedrich Stein把粥熬得很稀，一勺一勺吹凉，托着Gustav Winter的后颈喂下去。窗帘拉开半尺，太阳从床脚移到墙上，Gustav Winter第一次觉得退烧后的骨头是轻的。Friedrich Stein把药钱记在日历背面，说下个月少买两次肉。
  _effects: cash -2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); food; health 0.08 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_kin_care; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1948.03** `bc18-crime.failed-burglary::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/caught-in-darkroom` tags: crime, burglary, mistake, absurd, police, legal, comic, vienna
  显影液被Elisabeth Lechner 2碰翻，整盆药水泼在一张婚礼合影上。新郎的脸变成一团灰雾，店主抓住Gustav Winter的领子；Anton Moser来后，两人被关了一夜。第二天，Gustav Winter交了罚款三克朗，Elisabeth Lechner 2被新娘的家人追着要赔照片；那顶帽子也留在了拘留室。
  _effects: cash -3 (破坏暗房的罚款); reputation -0.040000000000000036; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:693; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144; condition; flag +destroyed_a_wedding_portrait; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1948.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Gustav Winter攥紧扶手。Emil Berg 2站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140_

  Josef Hofer把顶楼的小房间租给Gustav Winter，按钟点收钱，登记簿上要写两个人的关系。Gustav Winter说Rosa Kral是从林茨来的表妹；Josef Hofer看了她们一眼，把“表妹”写得很大，又在旁边添了一句“短住”。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:736; cash -2 (the room, the coal and the landlady's discretion（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +rented_room_as_cousins; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1949.01** `r13-fam.seek-friend::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/shared-table` tags: task, social, friendship, loneliness, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Winter把两只手插在外套口袋里，在食堂尽头坐下，问对面的人能不能拼桌。Rosa Lechner 2把盐罐推过来，又把自己的面包掰了一半。两人从天气说到班次，直到洗碗的女人开始收凳子，Gustav Winter才发现自己一晚上没有看钟。
  _effects: cash -1 (食堂尽头空着一把椅子（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +made_a_friend_at_the_canteen; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; knowledge “the canteen is quiet after the second bell”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1949.04** `r2-leisure.kitchen-shortweight::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/told-the-housekeeper` tags: domestic_service, kitchen, theft, accounting, loyalty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Winter在楼梯口追上Hermann Berg 2，说称可能不准。Hermann Berg 2没回头，只说明天换一杆秤。第二天Gustav Winter被调去擦银器，Rosa Stein 2把一锅热水泼在门口，谁也没提黄油。
  _effects: reputation 0.040000000000000036; flag +reported_kitchen_shortweight; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; knowledge “the housekeeper will replace the butter scale without naming”; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

  Franz Stein听说Gustav Winter以前在厂里和工头吵过架，把钥匙收回口袋，说铺子不租给“惹事的人”。Gustav Winter站在门口，看见玻璃上自己的影子，背后是Josef刚擦干净的招牌。回家后，Gustav Winter把攒下的钱重新包进手帕。
  _effects: cash -2 (租下街角的空铺（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.12; condition; flag +refused_shop_lease; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1950.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Gustav Winter一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Emil Weiss 2送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  警察在午夜后闯进地下舞厅，把灯全部打开。Luise Kral让所有人靠墙站好，逐个记下名字和住址；Gustav Winter看见Grete Winter的鞋跟断了一只。第二天，名单上的一些名字出现在报纸的“风化事件”栏里。

  Gustav Winter和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Gustav Winter在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 55 -> 59; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 40 -> 37; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `r14-soc.club-election::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/disputed-the-count` tags: task, club_election, club, status, community, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Winter输了五票，要求把票重新数一遍。数到第二遍还是五票，Anna Berg把铁盒盖上，说“再数就难看了”。Gustav Winter说账本也得查，后厅一下子安静下来。那晚的合唱没有唱成。散会后，Anna Berg把铁盒抱在怀里，锁进酒馆老板的柜子。铁盒被放回柜子，账本却留在了桌上。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; reputation -0.04999999999999999; flag +disputed_club_election; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-141; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-141_

- **1950.08** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/three-days-only` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Gustav Winter只答应让Grete Winter住三天，腾出半张床，又托Rosa Winter去打听便宜床位。三天里Grete Winter白天出去找活，傍晚回来坐在行军床边上，藤箱始终没有打开。第三天晚上Rosa Winter带回消息，说有一处工人宿舍，一周一克朗；Gustav Winter数出一克朗硬币放在箱盖上，替Grete Winter垫了第一周。
  _effects: cash -1 (替表亲垫付工人宿舍第一周（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003988:child_

- **1950.10** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Gustav Winter和Luise Kral一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Gustav Winter去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Gustav Winter先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1951.01** `ld18-journalist.interview::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-story-is-bought` tags: press, interview, public_desire, scandal, money, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Katharina Berg 2把一沓钞票放在桌上，说要写“维也纳最勇敢的爱情故事”，但名字要改，旅馆要删。Gustav Winter数了数钱，够付半年房租；Grete Winter在旁边说不要卖，Gustav Winter说先活下去，再谈别的。
  _effects: cash 3 (the newspaper paid for a sanitised love story); state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 55 -> 70; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 50 -> 60; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 40 -> 45; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000584:child; flag +sold_the_story_

  办公室有人问Gustav Winter，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Gustav Winter说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Gustav Winter把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 37 -> 45; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 30 -> 26; reputation -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015126_

  道德协会寄来一封措辞客气的信，请Gustav Winter去“谈谈自己的生活方式”。她把信读完，折好，垫在桌脚下；桌子还是晃。第二天，她照常去上班，只是把头发剪短了一截，短到所有人都能看出她不在乎。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 0 -> 0; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.defiance 20 -> 28; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Gustav Winter最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Gustav Winter把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Gustav Winter说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 45 -> 51; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 26 -> 23; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Gustav Winter是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Gustav Winter在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 60 -> 56; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 70 -> 72; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: historical development:initiative-taxi:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2-evt:world-vienna-1912:008146-1945 as participant; knowledge “我参加了第二区夜班出租合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Ga。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.03** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Gustav Winter跑了一趟医院，把Josef Winter送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Gustav Winter回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999994 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +parent_hospitalized_

- **1951.06** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Gustav Winter只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Emil Winter手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Emil Winter把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Gustav Winter记下门牌，答应一早过去。

  衙门走廊的地砖是黑白相间的，Gustav Winter站着站着就把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。窗口里的办事员姓什么没人知道，只叫“三号窗”。表递进去时，里面说“等复核”，声音很平。

  _effects: cash -1.5 (赊煤到期还款含利息); food; flag +coal_credit_debt_

  有人在洗衣房说，Gustav Winter星期三下午进过一家旅馆，出来时头发是湿的。话传到市场，已经变成另一个版本；Rosa Lechner 2说得最细，连房间号都编好了。Gustav Winter没有解释，只把星期三的工票钉在厨房墙上。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:858; reputation -0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 38 -> 48; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +public_desire_active_

  办公室有人问Gustav Winter，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Gustav Winter说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Gustav Winter把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 48 -> 56; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 23 -> 19; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

  道德协会寄来一封措辞客气的信，请Gustav Winter去“谈谈自己的生活方式”。她把信读完，折好，垫在桌脚下；桌子还是晃。第二天，她照常去上班，只是把头发剪短了一截，短到所有人都能看出她不在乎。

- **1956.01** `ld18-breakup.returned-keys::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/burned-the-last-letter` tags: breakup, grief, love, keys, queer, ending, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Gustav Winter写了一封很长的信，写到天亮，最后只留下“保重”两个字。她把信烧了，灰倒进花盆；Rosa Kral来时，看见那盆天竺葵的叶子全黄了，问是不是浇多了水。Gustav Winter说可能是。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 75 -> 80; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 10; flag +burned_the_last_letter_

- **1956.01** `lc17-care.offers-to-care::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hires-the-neighbour` tags: care, family, aging, health, household, duty, vienna, urban
  Gustav Winter算了一夜账，第二天去敲楼下退休护士的门。看护每天下午来两小时，工钱按周付；Josef Winter嫌贵，Gustav Winter说“总比我辞工强”。第一周结束时，药盒上的时间第一次没有写错。
  _effects: state_add lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:982; cash -3 (the first week of paid care); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; flag +paid_for_parent_care_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Gustav Winter最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Gustav Winter把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Gustav Winter说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 80 -> 86; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 8 -> 5_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Gustav Winter是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Gustav Winter在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 76 -> 72; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 66 -> 68; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1956.01** `state_expire:oj18-odd-apprenticeship` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  Gustav Winter的「奇职学徒」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:859; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:office-under-siege/pressure-year/held-on` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  质询会开了两个小时，Gustav Winter把每一笔签字都念了一遍。没有人被说服，但也没有人再追问；散会时，支持Gustav Winter的人比进门时多了两个。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 65; state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 26; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1956.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Gustav Winter为第二区夜班出租合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsam出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-taxi:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2-evt:world-vienna-1912:008146-1945 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015_

- **1956.05** `state_reaction:lc17-caregiver/patient-death` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Gustav Winter去世的消息传来时，Gustav Winter正在把当天的药片分进小格。护士问要不要留一件衣服，Gustav Winter说“留着那件灰的”，说完才发现自己的声音很平，像在替别人办手续。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +buried_parent_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018787_

- **1956.06** `r13-rel.long-distance-marriage::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/money-order-arrived` tags: relationship, marriage, distance, letters, money, vienna, urban, postwar
  邮差送来一张汇款单，附言栏里只有“买煤”两个字。Gustav Winter把钱取出来，先交了房租，又给自己买了一双不漏水的鞋。晚上写信时，Gustav Winter写了很多，最后只寄出一句：家里都好。
  _effects: cash 4 (每月一封没有署名的信); flag +living_on_money_orders; role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1956.06** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-wedding-invitation` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  请柬上写着Gustav Winter的名字，旁边用铅笔注了一句“一个人来也可以”。婚礼上，新娘把手捧花直接递给她，笑着说“下一个该你了”；同桌的人都在等Gustav Winter怎么回答。她把花放在膝上，说这花很香。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 58 -> 66; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 48 -> 53; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018639_

- **1956.12** `bc18-inherit.parrot-condition::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/parrot-outlives` tags: inheritance, family, condition, absurd, death, legal, quiet, vienna
  姑妈的遗嘱只有一条：谁替她养那只灰鹦鹉满两年，谁就拿十二克朗。Gustav Winter搬进阁楼，每天煮谷子、换水、用旧报纸垫笼底。鹦鹉学会了学门铃响，也学会了在Gustav Winter咳嗽时叫“吃药”。两年后，公证人Anton Moser来验鸟，鹦鹉在他帽子上拉了一泡；Gustav Winter拿到钱，先买了一只新笼子。
  _effects: cash 12 (养满两年鹦鹉得到的遗产); skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; condition; flag +kept_the_parrot; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1957.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fined` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Luise Winter 2在第三份合同上停住了：同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是Gustav Winter。罚款单当场开出，金额不算大，但足够让下一季度的办公用品全部改成最便宜的牌子。Gustav Winter没有争辩，只在收据背面写了日期。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; mood -0.020000000000000018; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:1023; flag +fined_after_audit; knowledge “同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是我的。”_

  Gustav Winter从「无法登记的伴侣」转入另一段处境：the pressure made the attachment publicly legible
  _effects: state_transition ld18-impossible-attachment -> known-affair; reputation -0.1; flag +unregistered_attachment_exposed; flag -impossible_attachment_active; flag +known_affair_active_

  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Gustav Winter是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Gustav Winter在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 72 -> 68; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 68 -> 70; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1957.01** `state_expire:office-under-siege` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  Gustav Winter的「职位受围」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:938; flag -office_under_siege_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/carried` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Gustav Winter又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Josef Winter今年记得住Gustav Winter的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 20 -> 16; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 5 -> 3; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1957.01** `r13-work.strike-fund::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/soup-kitchen-opened` tags: work, union, strike, mutual_aid, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Winter把工会活动室的长桌拼起来，用两口大锅煮扁豆汤。Berta Berg赊了半袋豆子和一筐洋葱，条件是罢工结束后先还他。第一天来了三十七个人，Gustav Winter给每个碗里多舀半勺；汤不够时，就往锅里再添一壶水。
  _effects: cash -1 (罢工第七天的汤锅（其中1克朗记入债务）); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (罢工第七天的汤锅); flag +work13_strike_soup_kitchen; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; organization_funds_

- **1957.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Gustav Winter记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Hermann Winter说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Hermann Winter陪着Gustav Winter去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003283:child_

- **1957.05** `ld18-raid.private-club::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/the-owner-pays` tags: queer, raid, police, public, scandal, nightlife, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Helene Huber在门口和Luise Lechner低声说了几句，塞过去一只信封。警察走后，他转头对Gustav Winter说，下个月的酒钱要加一倍。Emil Winter问要不要换地方；Gustav Winter说先把这个月过完。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150; cash -1 (the raised bar bill after the bribe); state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 86 -> 91; flag +club_owner_paid_the_police; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150_

- **1957.05** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-question-at-the-office` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  办公室有人问Gustav Winter，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Gustav Winter说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Gustav Winter把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 91 -> 99; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 5 -> 1; reputation -0.02_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019379_

- **1957.07** `r2-law.lottery-ticket::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/small-prize` tags: finance, lottery, gambling, luck, neighbors, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Winter把开奖名单钉在柜台上，Gustav Winter的号码中了末等奖，够买两袋面粉。Gustav Winter把烟盒上的号码对了两遍，Josef Winter说末等奖不用去总行，在这里就能兑。Friedrich Stein在旁边说，先把上次借的面粉钱还了；Gustav Winter数出钱，剩下的刚好够买一张新彩票。
  _effects: cash 6 (中奖号码写在烟盒上); flag +won_lottery_consolation_prize; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; knowledge “consolation prizes can be redeemed at the sales agent”_
